### [1] [Hot off the Stacks: X-rays in Romulus25, Part I - Comparisons to Observations from eRosita](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37763)
*N. Nicole Sanchez,Andrew Benson,Gwen Rudie,Ben Oppenheimer,Hannah Bish,Emily Silich,Phil Hopkins,John ZuHone,Michael Tremmel,Tom Quinn*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用Romulus25宇宙学模拟对750多个星系的热环星系介质进行X射线正向建模，发现其表面亮度和X射线光度总体高于观测，但零金属丰度模型能显著改善与观测的吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRG/eROSITA全天巡天为研究银河系质量及以上星系周围的热环星系介质（CGM）提供了新观测约束，需要检验当前宇宙学模拟能否重现这些X射线观测结果。

Method: 从Romulus25模拟中选取750多个恒星质量在10.0 < log(M*/M☉) < 11.5 的星系，用pyXSIM和SIXTE正向模拟[0.5-2.0] keV软X射线观测，测量热CGM的表面亮度分布，按恒星质量分为六个子样本并与Chadayammuri et al. (2022a)、Zhang et al. (2024a)对比；同时计算各星系热CGM的X射线光度Lχ，与Zhang et al. (2024b)观测及EAGLE、TNG、SIMBA模拟比较，并测试零金属丰度发射模型。

Result: 堆叠的表面亮度分布整体比观测更亮，尤其在内侧CGM区域；平均Lχ在多数质量段高于观测，仅在银河系质量下限附近与观测及多数模拟较为一致。采用零金属丰度模型后（因Romulus25未包含金属线冷却），平均Lχ降低近一个数量级，与观测吻合更好，尤其在银河系质量区间。

Conclusion: Romulus25模拟的热CGM X射线辐射偏亮，金属丰度处理（缺乏金属线冷却）是导致差异的重要因素；引入零金属丰度发射模型可显著缓解与观测的不一致。

Abstract: Recent observations from the from Spectrum Roentgen Gamma (SRG)/eROSITA all-sky survey data have helped to illuminate the hot circumgalactic medium (CGM) around galaxies at Milky Way-masses and above. In our study, we explore the hot X-ray emitting (T \textgreater 10$^6$ K) component of the CGM in over 750 galaxies from the Romulus25 cosmological simulation (Tremmel et al. 2017) between 10.0 < log(M$_*/M_{\odot}$) < 11.5. We forward-model soft X-ray [0.5 - 2.0] keV observations of our simulated galaxies using pyXSIM and SIXTE to measure the X-ray surface brightness (SB) profiles of the hot CGM. We then compare the average X-ray surface brightness profiles of six stellar-mass selected sub-samples to compare to the observational results from Chadayammuri et al. (2022a) and Zhang et al. 2024a. Broadly, we find that our stacked SB profiles are too bright compared to observations from Chadayammuri et al. (2022a) and Zhang et al.(2024a) particularly in the inner CGM. Additionally, we calculate the X-ray luminosity (L$_χ$) of the hot CGM for each galaxy and compare the average L$_χ$ across stellar mass to observations from Zhang et al. (2024b) as well as measurements from the EAGLE, TNG, SIMBA simulations. Across stellar mass, the average L$_χ$ of galaxies from Romulus25 are brighter than observations except at the lower, Milky Way-mass end where we are in relative agreement with both observations and most other simulations. We also consider a zero-metallicity emission model, because Romulus25 does not have metal-line cooling, finding the average L$_χ$ decreases by nearly an order-of-magnitude, which better matches observations particularly in the Milky Way-mass regime.

</details>


### [2] [Resolving the thermal component of stellar feedback with the "anchor-and-refine" scheme in galactic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37767)
*Chengzhe Li,Hui Li,Wei Cui,Federico Marinacci,Laura Sales,Mark Vogelsberger,Paul Torrey*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 提出"锚定-细化"（anchor-and-refine）超新星反馈方案，通过锚定恒星粒子所在宿主网格并视冷却半径是否可解析选择热注入或细化后注入终端动量，从而在有限分辨率下保留热气泡内部结构与热/动能量分配，改善热气体、恒星形成与星系风的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系尺度模拟常无法解析超新星冷却半径，基于动量的亚网格模型因此难以正确处理年轻恒星周围的热气体，导致热反馈库被削弱、热气体处理不足。

Method: 设计锚定-细化反馈方案：在每个恒星粒子附近锚定一个宿主网格单元，防止其位移并保持热反馈储存库；若冷却半径可解析，则将全部超新星能量以热能形式注入宿主单元；否则细化宿主单元并向周围气体单元沉积终端动量。随后开展理想化测试与孤立银河系质量星系模拟，并进行相分解分析。

Result: 理想化测试与高分辨率模拟结果一致，超气泡性质相符，热内部得以保留且无人工冷却；在孤立银河系质量星系中捕捉到更精细的X射线发射结构及边界清晰的X射线明亮中央中面，恒星形成历史比纯混合反馈更平滑；外流质量负载由热气体主导，在z>1 kpc处质量负载因子稳定在约0.2；相分解显示热气体维持稳定的能量驱动风，而较冷外流因辐射冷却与引力回落随高度衰减。

Conclusion: 结果凸显热反馈在驱动星系风中的作用；通过保留热超气泡内部及热能与动能的分配，锚定-细化方法在有限分辨率下改善了热气体、恒星形成与反馈的处理，可支持未来星系尺度与宇宙学缩小模拟的应用。

Abstract: (abridged) Stellar feedback regulates galactic baryon cycles, but galaxy-scale simulations often cannot resolve supernova cooling radii. Momentum-based subgrid models can consequently underresolve the hot gas around young stars. We develop an "anchor-and-refine" feedback scheme that anchors a host cell near each star particle, preventing its displacement and preserving a thermal-feedback reservoir. When the cooling radius is resolved, all supernova energy is injected into the host cell as thermal energy; otherwise, the host cell is refined and terminal momentum is deposited into surrounding gas cells. Idealized tests agree with high-resolution simulations, yielding consistent superbubble properties and preserving hot interiors without artificial cooling. In an isolated Milky Way-mass galaxy, the scheme captures finer X-ray-emitting structures and a sharply defined, X-ray-bright central mid-plane. It also produces a smoother star formation history than pure hybrid feedback. Hot gas dominates outflow mass loading, with the mass-loading factor plateauing at approximately 0.2 at heights z>1 kpc. Phase decomposition indicates that hot gas sustains a steady, energy-driven wind, whereas cooler outflows decline with height because of radiative cooling and gravitational fallback. These results highlight the role of thermal feedback in driving galactic winds. By preserving hot superbubble interiors and the partition of thermal and kinetic energy, anchor-and-refine improves the treatment of hot gas, star formation, and feedback at limited resolution, supporting future applications to galaxy-scale and cosmological zoom-in simulations.

</details>


### [3] [Extra! Extra! X-ray Emission!: X-rays in Romulus25, Part II - Physical Drivers of X-ray Emission in the CGM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37769)
*N. Nicole Sanchez,Andrew Benson,Gwen Rudie,Ben Oppenheimer,Hannah Bish,John ZuHone,Michael Tremmel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Romulus25模拟及其PIONEER zoom-in样本，系统解析了银河系质量星系热环星系介质（CGM）中X射线辐射的来源物理机制，指出热气体质量由恒星反馈与AGN反馈的总能量注入共同决定，而温度与金属丰度共同调制软X射线谱，金属富集晕可能使X射线观测产生偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画银河系质量星系周围温度约10^6 K的热CGM对理解星系演化至关重要，但此前工作（系列第一篇）只测得X射线辐射率与热气体质量的强相关性，尚未厘清究竟是哪些物理过程决定热X射线气体的存在及其亮度，因此需要借助可控的模拟样本进行因果层面的解释。

Method: 基于Romulus25宇宙学模拟中L*星系样本，统计热CGM气体质量（T > 10^5.5 K）与X射线辐射率ε_χ的关系，并考察其与热气体平均金属丰度的次级相关性；进一步采用Sanchez et al. (2019)的PIONEER zoom-in银河系质量星系样本（遗传修正技术、与R25相同物理模型）对上述相关性进行解释与归因。

Result: 热CGM气体质量M_gas,T>10^5.5K与ε_χ之间存在强相关，并存在与热气体平均金属丰度的次级相关。热X射线气体的存在由两个关键过程决定：(1) 热气体量由恒星反馈与AGN反馈的总能量注入共同设定；(2) 热气体的平均温度与金属丰度共同改变软X射线谱，使得在相同热气体质量下，金属富集CGM更亮、贫金属CGM更暗。

Conclusion: 研究首次表明，金属富集的气体晕可能系统性偏倚银河系质量星系的X射线观测，提示在利用X射线辐射推断热CGM质量或反馈能量时须考虑金属丰度选择效应；同时强调恒星反馈与AGN反馈需联合考虑才能正确解读热CGM。

Abstract: Characterizing the hot (T ~ 10$^{6}$ K) circumgalactic medium (CGM) around Milky Way-mass (MW-mass) galaxies is critical to a full understanding of galaxy evolution. In this second paper of the "X-rays in Romulus25" series, we unpack the specific physical processes giving rise to the presence of hot X-ray emitting gas in the halos of galaxies from the Romulus25 (R25) simulation, focusing on L$_{*}$ galaxies. We determine that a strong correlation appears between the mass of hot CGM gas, M$_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$, and the X-ray emissivity, $ε_χ$, measured in the first paper of this series, (Sanchez et al. 2026a, submitted). There is also a secondary correlation between M$_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$ and $ε_χ$ with the average metallicity of the hot CGM gas, $\bar{Z}_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$. We use the well-studied PIONEER simulations from Sanchez et al. (2019), a set of zoom-in MW-mass galaxies run with the genetic modification technique of Roth et al. (2016) and the same physics as R25, to interpret these correlations. We determine that the presence of hot X-ray emitting gas is determined by two key processes: 1) the quantity of hot gas mass in the CGM, M$_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$, is set by the combined total energy injection of $both$ stellar and AGN feedback, and 2) together the average temperature and metallicity of the hot CGM changes the soft X-ray spectrum of the gas resulting in brighter metal-rich CGM and dimmer metal-poor CGM for the same quantity of M$_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$. These results indicate, for the first time, that metal-rich gas halos may bias X-ray observations of MW-mass galaxies.

</details>


### [4] [Not so dead after all: young stars in the massive S0 NGC 3115](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37774)
*João P. V. Benedetti,Alexandre Vazdekis,Anna Ferré-Mateu,Elham Eftekhari,Alberto Rodríguez-Ardila,Rogério Riffel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用SOAR/Goodman光谱与谱线强度拟合，测得大质量透镜星系NGC 3115中心年轻恒星质量分数为0.69%，并在1 kpc外归零，表明"红而死"星系中残余恒星形成普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早型星系传统上被视为"红而死"（red-and-dead）系统，但紫外光谱可揭示质量分数不足1%的隐藏年轻星族。此前研究多集中于椭圆星系，尚不清楚该成分在其它类型早型星系中是否普遍存在及其空间分布如何。

Method: 采用SOAR/Goodman光谱与谱线强度（line-strength）拟合，以"单一主体星族+年轻成分（过去1 Gyr内恒定恒星形成）"建模恒星形成历史；利用ultranest采样获得年龄、金属丰度、[Mg/Fe]及年轻恒星质量分数的后验分布；并通过U波段指数随空间位置的变化刻画年轻成分的分布。同时对比了极端水平分支星等其它紫外偏亮模型的拟合效果。

Result: 年轻恒星质量分数在星系中心达到峰值0.69^{+0.09}_{-0.15}%，在1 kpc之外降为零。该年轻成分与运动学结构相关，在压强支撑区域占主导。可解释为内部过程（如恒星物质再循环）驱动的残余中心恒星形成。替代模型（如极端水平分支星）拟合更差，且需要不符合物理的质量分数。

Conclusion: 年轻恒星在年老的"红而死"星系中比预期更为常见；其在不同早型星系中呈现相似的梯度分布，说明残余恒星形成是这类星系的普遍特征，为理解其重子循环提供了关键约束。

Abstract: Context: Massive early-type galaxies (ETGs) are traditionally considered "red-and-dead" based on optical observations. However, ultraviolet (UV) spectroscopy can uncover a hidden population of young stars with a sub-one-per cent mass fraction. Aims: We measure the young stellar mass fraction in NGC 3115, a massive isolated lenticular galaxy, to test the ubiquity and spatial distribution of this component beyond previously studied ellipticals. Methods: Using SOAR/Goodman spectra and line-strength fitting, we modelled the star-formation history with a single bulk stellar population plus a young component (constant star-formation over the last 1 Gyr). We used ultranest to derive posterior distributions for age, metallicity, [Mg/Fe], and the young stellar mass fraction. Results: By tracking spatial variations in U-band indices, we found the young stellar mass fraction peaks at $0.69^{+0.09}_{-0.15}\%$ in the centre, dropping to zero beyond 1 kpc. This young component correlates with kinematic structures, dominating in the pressure-supported region. We interpret this as residual central star formation fueled by internal processes, such as recycled stellar material. Alternative UV-bright models (e.g., extreme horizontal branch stars) yielded poorer fits and unphysically high mass fractions. Conclusions: Young stars in old, "red-and-dead" galaxies are more common than expected. Their presence across various ETGs with similar gradients indicates that residual star formation is a ubiquitous feature, offering key constraints on the baryon cycle in these galaxies.

</details>


### [5] [Ejected and Rejected: Strong Scatterings in Dense Star Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37775)
*Adam Distler,Nicholas C. Stone*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文系统推导了球对称各向同性分布函数下强散射（近距离遭遇）造成的速度踢精确微分速率分布、分布边界与近似标度关系，并将其应用于恒星抛射与EMRI"取消"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞恒星系统（球状星团与核星团）的演化主要由弱小角散射决定，但罕见强散射可能带来定性不同的结果，其天体物理重要性尚缺乏系统量化。

Method: 在代表球状星团与核星团的各向同性球对称分布函数下，推导速度踢的精确微分速率分布，给出分布边界与近似速率标度；再推广到核星团中能量与角动量踢的近似速率，并应用于(i)恒星抛射与(ii)EMRI形成前体的取消。

Result: 抛射速率在某些区间因强踢分布饱和与弱极限失效而变得复杂，可能增强损失锥屏蔽效应；相当比例的"原初EMRI"可能被强散射取消。

Conclusion: 强散射在损失锥相关过程中不可忽略，需要未来工作进一步确定其对真实EMRI速率的定量影响。

Abstract: Understanding the evolution of collisional stellar systems is key to predicting both the bulk evolution of dense star clusters and also the rates of tidal disruption events, extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs), stellar ejections, and other loss cone phenomena. Although a majority of the evolution is dictated by weak, small-angle scatterings, rare close encounters resulting in strong scatterings can sometimes produce qualitatively different outcomes. To systematically determine the astrophysical importance of strong scatterings, we derive the exact differential rate distribution of velocity kicks in isotropic and spherically symmetric distribution functions representing globular and nuclear star clusters, while also deriving the distribution boundaries and approximate rate scalings. We then extend this formalism to derive approximate rates of energy and angular momentum kicks for objects in the nuclear star cluster, and then apply this framework to study (i) ejections and (ii) ``cancellation'' of extreme mass ratio inspirals. We find that ejection rates are complicated in some regimes due to the saturation of the strong kick distribution and the breakdown of the weak limit, likely enhancing the effect of loss cone shielding. We also determine that a sizable fraction of proto-EMRIs will likely be canceled by strong scatterings, highlighting the need for future work to determine to what degree strong scatterings impact the true EMRI rate.

</details>


### [6] [Simulation-Informed Dust Masses in Typical $6<z<14$ JWST Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37821)
*Jed McKinney,Dhruv T. Zimmerman,Desika Narayanan,Caitlin M. Casey,Julian B. Munoz,Paul Torrey,Hui Li,Federico Marinacci*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 提出利用恒星半光半径与紫外/光学衰减推断高红移星系尘埃质量的新方法，并应用于JWST观测星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型z>8星系的尘埃直接探测仍不可行，但静止系紫外/光学光谱测光暗示存在尘埃，需要间接可靠的质量推断手段。

Method: 基于尘埃粒子实时演化的宇宙学zoom-in模拟，建立以恒星半光半径和UV/光学衰减为输入的尘埃质量定标关系，并用于6<z<14的JWST星系。

Result: 方法复现低红移观测趋势，但与基于银河系的定标因子偏离可达约1.5 dex；观测星系尘埃质量为10^3–10^5太阳质量，与ALMA上限一致。

Conclusion: 高红移尘埃可能由超新星早期富集后被热气体中高效破坏；所提供定标表可用于大范围恒星质量与红移下的尘埃质量推断。

Abstract: Direct detections of dust in typical $z>8$ galaxies remain untenable, despite the presence of dust suggested by rest-frame UV/optical spectro-photometry. Informed by cosmological zoom-in simulations where dust particles evolve on-the-fly, we present a novel method for inferring dust masses in high-redshift galaxies using stellar half-light radii and UV/optical attenuation. Our methods reproduce observed trends at lower redshift, ultimately diverging from Milky Way-based calibration factors by up to $\sim1.5$ dex. We apply this method to observed JWST galaxies from $6<z<14$, and find dust masses in the range $M_{\rm dust}\sim10^3-10^5\,M_\odot$ that are fully consistent with upper limits from ALMA, and suggest an early dust enrichment period by supernovae followed by efficient grain destruction in the hot gas. Future observations may use our tabulated calibration to infer dust masses over a wide range of stellar masses and redshifts.

</details>


### [7] [No Significant Excess in Typical Variability in CLAS+ Changing-Look AGN Candidates in Rubin DP2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37876)
*Lilianne Nakazono*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用Rubin DP2对约1000–9000天后的CLAS+变化型视界AGN候选体进行光学测光检验，未发现其短期变异性显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLAS+候选体在光谱转变后是否仍保持增强的短期光学变异性尚不清楚；Rubin DP2的观测时间跨度恰好可在转变后较晚时期检验这一问题。

Method: 将CLAS+候选体（按turn-on、turn-off、intermediate三种状态分组）与其对比历元后约1000–9000天的DP2测光数据匹配，逐波段比较峰峰振幅与光变宁静类星体对照样本，并检验中位振幅及中位|Δmag|随时间延迟的差异。

Result: 两个波段下各状态均未检出显著的中位振幅超出，也未发现中位|Δmag|随时间延迟的系统差异；r波段CLAS+振幅分布比对照更宽，其中intermediate状态经多重比较校正后显著。

Conclusion: 转变后未见短期光学变异性整体增强，但r波段更宽的振幅分布（尤其intermediate状态显著）暗示可能存在高振幅子群体，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Rubin Data Preview 2 (DP2) samples the CLAS+ changing-look AGN (CLAGN) candidates $\sim1,000-9,000$ days after their comparison epochs, so it can test whether these objects retain enhanced short-term optical variability after their spectral transitions. Comparing peak-to-peak amplitude in magnitudes against a photometrically quiescent quasar control, one band at a time, we do not detect a significant median-amplitude excess for any state (turn-on, turn-off, intermediate) in either of two bands, nor a different in the median $\,|\,Δ\text{mag}\,|\,$ as function of time lag. The $r$-band CLAS+ amplitude distributions are wider than the control's, with the intermediate state formally significant after multiple-comparison correction. This could hint for a high-amplitude subpopulation.

</details>


### [8] [The Carousel Lens III: Joint lens convergence and source kinematic modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37971)
*William Sheu,Xiaosheng Huang,Tania M. Barone,Fuyan Bian,Aleksandar Cikota,Karl Glazebrook,Tesla Jeltema,Evan Odell,Jackson H. O'Donnell,Linus Upson,Felipe Urcelay,Sean Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的积分场光谱数据立方体正演建模方法，将透镜弧中的空间分辨运动学作为独立约束，应用于Carousel Lens系统，显著收紧爱因斯坦半径与斜率的不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

These results establish datacube-level forward modeling as a practical and independent route to traditional image-based lens models in the current systematics-limited regime, and one that extends naturally to this system's other lensed sources and to other lens systems.

The emerging bars seen in the $f_{\rm gas}= 80\%$ and $100\%$ simulations collapse in $\lesssim$1 Gyr to form central bulges supported by rotation, and this collapse follows soon after the chaotic fraction starts to dominate ($f_{\rm chaos} \gtrsim 50\%)$. Moreover, the gravitational potential in those simulations steepens rapidly over that period, and by integrating the chaotic orbits, we find that orbits that deviate from the evolving mean field are more likely to be chaotic. Hence, we argue that the irregularity associated with the rapidly evolving, central potentials drives higher chaotic fractions in these gas-rich bars. Abstract: We present near-infrared K-band ($1.94$--$2.52~μ$m) spectroscopy of the deeply embedded protostellar source HOPS\,370, obtained with the TIFR--ARIES Near Infrared Spectrometer (TANSPEC) on the 3.6-m Devasthal Optical Telescope (DOT). The spectrum is dominated by prominent molecular hydrogen ($\rm H_2$) ro-vibrational emission, with multiple transitions detected from the $1$--$0$ S, $2$--$1$ S, and $1$--$0$ Q branches. Using the intrinsically linked $\rm H_2$ $1$--$0$ Q(3) and $1$--$0$ S(1) transitions, which arise from the same upper energy level, we derive a visual extinction of $A_V = 23.24 \pm 1.25$ mag, corresponding to $A_K = 2.50 \pm 0.13$ mag, consistent with the embedded Class~0/I nature of the source. The diagnostic line ratio $F(2$--$1~{\rm S}(1))/F(1$--$0~{\rm S}(1)) = 0.12 \pm 0.01$ is consistent with thermal excitation and disfavors ultraviolet fluorescence as the dominant excitation mechanism. Assuming optically thin emission and local thermodynamic equilibrium, we derive an $\rm H_2$ excitation diagram that yields an excitation temperature of $2078 \pm 215$ K and a molecular hydrogen column density of $(1.93 \pm 0.93)\times10^{20}$ cm$^{-2}$. We further derive an ortho-to-para ratio of $2.45 \pm 0.21$, indicating substantial shock-driven thermal processing while remaining below the high-temperature equilibrium value of 3. Taken together, these diagnostics indicate that the molecular hydrogen emission in HOPS\,370 originates predominantly in warm, shock-heated gas most likely associated with its protostellar outflow. Our results demonstrate the diagnostic power of combined S- and Q-branch $\rm H_2$ spectroscopy for constraining the physical conditions and excitation history of deeply embedded protostellar systems.

Abstract: Determining how the Milky Way warp evolves provides constraints on the formation and dynamical evolution of galactic disks. Using Apache Point Observatory Galactic Evolution Experiment Data Release 19 (APOGEE DR19) spectroscopy, we estimate ages for 222,355 red giants. With Gaia astrometry and geometric distances, we analyze 168,824 of these stars in five age bins spanning $0$--$10$ Gyr. The warp-related radial vertical-velocity ($V_Z$) profile of young red giants ($0$--$2$ Gyr) is broadly consistent with those of classical Cepheids and young open clusters. Over $9\leq R<12$ kpc, we find that the mean $V_Z$ values of the intermediate-age red giants ($2$--$4$ and $4$--$6$ Gyr) are $1.48\pm0.46$ and $1.49\pm0.52$ km s$^{-1}$ higher than that of the young population, respectively. By contrast, at $R\simeq12$--$13$ kpc, the $V_Z$ profiles of the $0$--$6$ Gyr red giants are consistent within their uncertainties. The intermediate-age enhancement persists across our robustness tests and may reflect smaller effective kinematic warp onset radii for the intermediate-age populations. The observed velocity differences may arise from the combined effects of earlier perturbations and the present-day warp, or from differences in vertical structure among stellar populations.

Abstract: Most existing idealized simulations of galaxy mergers neglect Hubble expansion, as the local Hubble flow is curved by overdensities and the expansion effect is weak at these scales. However, even in the gravity-dominated Local Group (LG), the expansion itself still persists, and it has been shown important in some LG-related studies. Here we take the future merger of the Milky Way (MW) and Andromeda (M31) as a case study to investigate the differences between simulations with and without Hubble expansion. We perform a suite of idealized N-body simulations of the MW--M31 system under pure Newtonian, $Λ$CDM, and $w_0w_a$CDM scenarios, testing different masses and proper motions of galaxies informed by current observational constraints. At low M31 tangential velocities, especially in cases where the two galaxies merge within a Hubble time, the expansion effect is negligible; while at high velocities (>66.5 km/s), the $Λ$CDM simulations introduce a noticeable delay in the merger time compared to the Newtonian ones and may even alter the final fate of the system, but the caveat here is that the isolated-pair idealization and the assumed expansion history break down. The delay is also present but less pronounced in the $w_0w_a$CDM runs. When M33 and the Large Magellanic Cloud are included, the differences in merger times are reduced. In summary, Hubble expansion can play a noticeable role for galaxy mergers with high tangential velocities, but its actual influence on the MW--M31 merger depends on the precise masses and proper motions to be determined in the future.

NewCluster shows no clear monotonic evolution either, although it exhibits larger scatter, particularly toward high host masses.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度成像巡天（如ARRAKIHS、LSST、Euclid）正发现越来越多低表面亮度恒星流，这些流可追溯被瓦解矮星系前身的轨道演化和内部结构。需要系统理解在ΛCDM框架下矮星系潮汐特征的演化规律及其在不同表面亮度极限下的可探测性，以指导观测解释。

Method: 对绕行于静态类银河系势场（含盘、核球和暗物质晕）的矮星系进行高分辨率N体模拟。每个矮星系由嵌入尖（cuspy）或核（cored）广义NFW暗物质晕中的Plummer恒星分布构成。在10 Gyr内每0.5 Gyr生成理想化积分表面亮度图，并通过亮度高于各极限表面亮度（μlim=29–32 mag/arcsec²，对应ARRAKIHS、LSST、Euclid深度）的投影面积量化可探测性。

Each dwarf comprised a Plummer stellar distribution embedded in a cuspy or cored generalised Navarro--Frenk--White dark matter halo. Idealised integrated surface brightness maps were constructed every 0.5 Gyr over 10 Gyr, and detectability was quantified through the projected area of stellar light brighter than limiting surface brightnesses, $μ_{\rm lim}=29$--$32$ mag arcsec$^{-2}$, chosen to span depths relevant to ARRAKIHS, LSST, and Euclid. The detectability of tidal features depends most strongly on progenitor stellar mass, orbital configuration, parameterised by the eccentricity proxy $e_0$, and evolutionary stage. Across this range, extended tidal debris is predominantly recovered for initially more radial configurations, mainly those with $e_0 \gtrsim 0.75$. In the simulated grid, detectable area is dominated by $M_\star=10^8$--$10^9 M_\odot$ progenitors, whereas $M_\star=10^6$--$10^7 M_\odot$ systems can undergo stripping without detectable debris. The detectable area evolves non-monotonically with time, reflecting the competition between stream formation, phase mixing, and the fading of diffuse debris below the adopted surface brightness limits. Abstract: While numerous studies have shown that satellite galaxies experience declining star formation rates after infall, direct comparisons across different surveys and redshifts are complicated by varying sample selections, environmental metrics, and techniques used to derive galaxy properties. In this work, we select satellite galaxies from the highly complete Galaxy and Mass Assembly (GAMA) Survey and the Deep Extragalactic VIsible Legacy Survey (DEVILS) to investigate environmental quenching out to z~0.4. These surveys derive galaxy properties and environments in a consistent manner, minimizing systematic biases and enabling a robust comparison across redshift. Using projected phase space as a proxy for infall time, we quantify the evolution of the quenched fraction and trace how satellites evolve relative to the star-forming main sequence (SFMS). We find consistent trends between GAMA and DEVILS, with satellites exhibiting a steady decline in star formation relative to the SFMS at a rate of ~0.14 dex/Gyr following infall. Extending this analysis to include visual morphology, we find similar declining trends for both early- and late-type systems. Overall, we find no significant redshift evolution between GAMA and DEVILS, indicating that group and cluster environments have a comparable impact on the star formation activity of satellite galaxies over the past ~4.5 Gyr.

Abstract: Supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHBs) are expected products of massive galaxy mergers, but identifying binaries in nearby galaxies remains challenging. We combine simulated massive-galaxy assembly histories from IllustrisTNG with a semi-analytic core-scouring model to connect observable core galaxy properties to the probability of hosting a bound SMBHB at z=0. We (i) construct a core galaxy population using an observationally motivated galaxy stellar mass function, (ii) model binary evolution through dynamical friction, stellar hardening, and gravitational-wave emission, and (iii) perform logistic regressions with five observable predictors: stellar mass deficit, stellar mass, central density, central velocity dispersion, and effective radius. The resulting models perform well on simulated systems, achieving a pseudo-$R^2$ of $0.51\pm0.03$, accuracy $0.95\pm0.01$, precision $0.92\pm0.03$, and recall $0.82\pm0.04$. Uncoalesced binaries are typically compact, with separations peaking near 0.3 pc, though only $\sim0.3\%$ emit in the pulsar timing array band. Applying the model to observed core galaxies, we identify eight nearby galaxies more likely than not to host SMBHBs. Of those eight host candidates, we note Messier 86 (NGC 4406) as a particularly likely example that was similarly identified using independent means and therefore warrants future follow-up.

Abstract: A variety of faint and observationally challenging stellar systems has been discovered in the Milky Way in the last decades. Unveiling their kinematic and chemical properties is crucial to understand their nature and to assess their role in the history of our Galaxy. Specifically, we provide the first spectroscopic characterization of metallicity and α-element content for BH 140, the first comprehensive chemical characterization for BLISS 1 and Garro 01, and the first spectroscopic metallicity determination for Kim 3. BH 140, Garro 01 and BLISS 1 are relatively metal-rich systems with [Fe/H] ~ -0.7 dex and have enhanced α-element abundances, whereas Kim 3 is notably metal-poor with [Fe/H] ~ -1.8 dex. We also performed an orbital analysis to investigate their origin as accreted or in situ systems. We found that Garro 01 and BH 140 have orbits confined within < 1.5 kpc from the Galactic plane, the former having also a remarkably circular orbit. They were likely born in situ, although the high orbital eccentricity of BH 140 and its mild deficiency in α-elements leave other possibilities open. Although undoubtedly accreted, BLISS 1 does not exhibit a clear dynamical association with any known progenitor, lying in a high energy (E) - high vertical angular momentum (LZ ) prograde orbit reaching a maximum height from the Galactic plane of zmax ~ 8 kpc. Finally, we tentatively link the high energy and low LZ orbit of Kim 3 to the Elqui stream.

Here we report measurements of portions of the centimeter, millimeter, and sub-millimeter regions of the rotational spectrum of the most stable conformer of 3-methoxy-1-propanol in order to probe the presence of even larger methoxylated alcohols toward the source. The experimental rotational parameters determined from these spectra were used to simulate the rotational spectrum of 3-methoxy-1-propanol under the physical conditions of the MM1 source and compared to the same ALMA observational dataset of the source in which 2-methoxyethanol was first discovered. No 3-methoxy-1-propanol transitions are identified in the data, and an upper limit on its column density toward the source was determined to be $7.5 \cdot 10^{15} \text{ cm}^{-2}$. When considering the series of these three methoxy alcohols, the decrease in column density going from 2-methoxyethanol to 3-methoxy-1-propanol is consistent with how the column density of 2-methoxyethanol decreases compared to methoxymethanol.

We examined the galaxy's FUV extent and compared it with the H I distribution. We extracted thirty SFCs and derived their properties, including area, luminosity, and star formation rate (SFR), and compared them with previous studies. FUV-emitting stars and their stellar parameters were identified through cross-matching with catalogs from the Hubble Space Telescope and the Multi Unit Spectroscopic Explorer (MUSE). We also estimated the global SFR and star formation efficiencies (SFEs) associated with previously identified CO clouds. The FUV luminosities of the SFCs confirm the absence of massive O and B-type stars in Leo T. The FUV-emitting stellar population consists of main-sequence (MS) stars, blue loop (BL) stars, horizontal branch stars, and emission-line (EM) stars. The upper MS and EM stars are concentrated near the optical center, whereas the lower MS and BL stars are more spatially extended. We find that five out of the six FUV-emitting stars with MUSE spectroscopy are extremely metal-poor ([Fe/H] $\leq$ -3). The (NUV - r) color profile indicates that the UV emission is dominated by stellar populations younger than 1 Gyr out to a radius $\lt$ 185 pc. In the first, galaxies that initially form within massive dark matter halos lose the majority of their dark matter and transition into DMDGs (acquired DMDGs). In the second, galaxies form with little or no dark matter from the beginning (inborn DMDGs). The two populations show distinct physical properties. Acquired DMDGs exhibit significant temporal variations in mass content, size, and kinematics, whereas inborn DMDGs show mild evolution. The former population tends to form closer to their nearest neighbor galaxies in large-scale overdense regions than the latter population does. We find that most stars in the inborn DMDGs form in situ. Their precursor gas is already metal-enriched and associated with other galaxies before formation. Tidal interactions and filamentary gas flows may contribute to the assembly of compact gas clumps, suggesting multiple formation pathways for inborn DMDGs.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: A gravitationally-bound system of wide binary stars with precise 3D relative displacement $\mathbf{r}$ and velocity $\mathbf{v}$ can allow a Keplerian orbit reconstruction for an assumed gravitational parameter $G$. A sufficiently large sample of such systems can also be used to directly infer $G$ specific to the sample. When the sky-plane angular coordinates are (essentially) exact and the distances to the binary stars are sufficiently precise, the orbit modeling can be readily done using two internal consistency relations provided by the ratio of angular separations in R.A.\ and decl.\ and the physical sky-plane separation $s$ that must be satisfied by any Keplerian orbits. A recent study argued that treating $a$ explicitly free with an empirical prior imposed on it can dramatically change the inference of $G$ in the Chae et al.\ sample of 36 wide binaries. Here I introduce and test a Bayesian MCMC algorithm that allows $a$ to be explicitly free, and find using the $α$-Cen AB - proxima system and the Chae et al.\ sample that the explicit-$a$ algorithm with an observational constraint is fully equivalent to the implicit-$a$ algorithm with an internal consistency relation involving $s$. For the $α$-Cen AB - proxima system, I present orbits in Milgromian (MOND) as well as Newtonian gravity models obtained with the verified algorithms. I show that a recent claim of Newtonian gravity from the Chae et al.\ sample may be a consequence of using arbitrarily enlarged errors for some of $\mathbf{r}$ and/or $\mathbf{v}$. I discuss how wide binaries can be properly analyzed and modeled to probe gravity at low acceleration and test gravity models, including the necessity of deriving individual-specific parameters, the importance of follow-up observations for anomalous systems, and the relevance of Bayesian MCMC approaches.

Abstract: Hoag-type galaxies contain a smooth central core surrounded by a detached outer ring, but their rarity has limited previous studies mainly to individual systems. We identify thirty galaxies with apparent Hoag-type morphology in DESI imaging by visually inspecting two machine-learning ring-galaxy catalogues and analyse their core and ring properties using DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys $g$, $r$, and $z$ images. We measure the rings after subtracting two-dimensional core models and compare the cores with matched SDSS galaxies. The cores lie predominantly on the red sequence, whereas the rings span a broader colour range extending toward the blue cloud. The cores broadly follow early-type mass scaling relations, but their mass-matched distributions tend towards slightly bluer colours, smaller sizes, higher stellar-mass surface densities, and lower concentrations and Sérsic indices. Of the 16 systems with SDSS group information, 14 (87.5 per cent) are centrals, compared with $\sim64\%$ in the mass-matched early-type control sample. The halo-mass and large-scale overdensity distributions become much more similar after central--satellite status is also matched. Ring stellar-mass surface density and normalized width decrease with increasing core-plus-inner-halo to ring mass ratio. Most core $R_{90}$ values lie inside the outermost stable circular orbit of a simplified core-plus-ring model, while the rings frequently extend across its unstable interval. The slightly bluer core colours, lower concentrations, and lower Sérsic indices suggest a possible secular contribution, but the compact, dense cores imply more complex structural assembly than a simple diffuse-pseudobulge origin. The high central fraction may favour gas retention and ring survival by reducing satellite-specific environmental processing. The diversity of ring properties nevertheless leaves room for multiple formation pathways.

Abstract: Only five out of the $\sim$350 molecules detected in the interstellar medium (ISM) carry the isocyanate functional group (NCO), the essential substructure of peptide bonds in proteins. Among them are isocyanic acid (HNCO) and its most stable N-protonated isomer H$_2$NCO$^+$. Previous studies have predicted that the O-protonated isomer HNCOH$^+$ should be present in the ISM; yet this species has remained elusive. We search for the metastable HNCOH$^+$ towards the Galactic Center molecular cloud G+0.693-0.027, where H$_2$NCO$^+$ has already been detected. We use an ultra-deep broadband spectral survey carried out towards G+0.693-0.027 with the Yebes 40-m, IRAM 30-m and APEX telescopes covering nearly 150 GHz of bandwidth between 31 and 275 GHz. We report the first detection of HNCOH$^+$ in the ISM with a column density of $N$ = (9.9 $\pm$ 0.5)$\times$10$^{12}$ cm$^{-2}$, which corresponds to a molecular abundance relative to H$_2$ of (7.3$^{+1.3} _{-1.0}$)$\times$10$^{-11}$. With the measured H$_2$NCO$^+$ column density of $N$ = (2.6 $\pm$ 0.3) $\times 10^{12}$ cm$^{-2}$, this yields a HNCOH$^+$/H$_2$NCO$^+$ ratio of 3.8$^{+0.5} _{-0.4}$. This result is consistent with recent kinetic calculations that favour the formation of HNCOH$^+$ over H$_2$NCO$^+$. The detection of HNCOH$^+$ in the ISM broadens the inventory of molecules known to contain the isocyanate functional group and reinforces recent works showing that isomer abundances in the ISM do not necessarily correlate with thermodynamic stability.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移（z~5）星系的尘埃质量、尘埃-金属比与消光曲线观测解释存在不确定性，需厘清早期宇宙中尘埃如何在低金属丰度、化学年轻的环境中生长与演化，并检验现有尘埃模型的适用性。

Method: 采用主模拟的spin-off宇宙学zoom-in模拟，耦合含两种颗粒组分（硅酸盐与碳质）、两个粒径尺度的"活"尘埃模型，追踪演化并计算消光曲线、尘埃-金属比与耗竭模式，与观测对比。

Abstract: We investigate dust evolution in galaxies at $z\sim5$ using a spin-off run of the \NC\ cosmological zoom-in simulation with a live two-species, two-size dust model. We find that dust evolution follows three metallicity-dependent phases. At $\log(Z/Z_\odot)<-1$, dust is dominated by stellar ejecta and produces a shallow, featureless UV extinction curve whose normalized wavelength dependence is similar to that of the Calzetti attenuation law (Phase I). At $-1\leq \log(Z/Z_\odot)<-0.5$, efficient silicate accretion drives rapid dust growth, producing silicate-rich dust and SMC-like extinction curves (Phase II). At $\log(Z/Z_\odot)\geq-0.5$, carbonaceous grain growth also becomes efficient, yielding a more balanced grain mixture and Milky-Way-like extinction curves (Phase III). In addition, local dust-to-metal ratios are strongly suppressed in regions affected by recent clustered supernova feedback, further complicating the interpretation of high-$z$ observations. Despite some successes, our galaxies lie below current observationally inferred dust masses and dust-to-metal ratios for high-redshift galaxies. The resulting depletion patterns suggest that the fixed Fe-bearing olivine composition assumed in our current dust model may be too restrictive for chemically young galaxies, and that metallicity-dependent grain compositions may be required to explain the dust content of the early Universe.

Abstract: Bulge globular clusters are key tracers of the complex formation history of the Galactic bulge, as they retain information on its past star-formation episodes. Their chemical abundances serve as empirical `fossil records'' of the early evolution of the bulge. We present a homogeneous analysis of archival high-resolution UVES spectra for 135 giants in 14 Galactic bulge globular clusters. We explore the results in the context of the phenomenon of multiple stellar populations and discuss what the abundances reveal about the chemical evolution of the bulge. We employed the CHEmical Survey analysis System (CHESS) pipeline to derive atmospheric parameters on an accurate scale defined by reference stars. Abundances for Li, O, Na, Mg, Al, Si, Ca, Sc, Y, Zr, Ba, Ce, Nd, and Eu were derived by spectrum synthesis. Deviations from local thermodynamic equilibrium (i.e., non-LTE or NLTE) were taken into account for Na, Mg, Al, Si, Ca, Y, Ba, and Eu. We serendipitously discovered a Li-rich giant with A(Li) = 1.68 (after 3D NLTE corrections) in NGC 6723, which was hidden in the archival data. Our sample includes both first- and second-generation stars, as indicated by the observed spread in Na abundances. Compared to halo clusters, the bulge clusters show a lower [α/Fe] plateau at low metallicity and a more gradual decline in [α/Fe] toward higher metallicities. The Sc abundances are below solar and lower than in disk stars of the same metallicity. This difference could be important for solving open questions about the nucleosynthesis of this element. None of the clusters exhibit any significant spreads in heavy elements. Most clusters exhibit [Ba/Eu] \sim 0.0, indicating a contribution from both s- and r-processes. Clusters with [Fe/H] < -0.5 show negative [Ce/Nd] ratios, supporting the idea that these elements were predominantly produced by the r-process in these clusters.

Abstract: The circumgalactic medium (CGM) of massive high-redshift galaxies contains inflowing filamentary gas, recycled material, and feedback-driven outflows. We investigate the formation and evolution of dense CGM clumps in two cosmological zoom-in simulations of the same massive halo at $z > 6$: the fiducial Fable model and the modified Fable-SE model, which includes enhanced early black hole growth through mild super-Eddington accretion. We identify dense gas clumps in the CGM and follow their gas properties, stellar content, and radial evolution across cosmic time. At the resolution probed here, the two simulations show broadly similar distributions of clump gas mass, gas size and associated stellar mass. Instead, the clump populations differ mainly in their abundance, metallicity, and radial evolution. Fable-SE produces substantially more metal-enriched clumps, including an outward-moving population during the strong AGN-driven blow-out phase at $z \lesssim 7$. These systems are associated with disturbed HI-rich gas and relatively high gas densities, linking the increased prevalence of dense, metal-enriched CGM structures in Fable-SE to its stronger AGN-driven blow-out phase. Although inward migration dominates overall, Fable-SE also contains an outward-moving clump population during the blow-out phase, with some clumps moving beyond the virial radius. Our results therefore indicate that the distribution of dense CGM clumps around massive high-redshift galaxies reflects both filamentary accretion and feedback-driven CGM evolution, with strong early AGN activity associated with the outward migration of dense, metal-enriched gas structures containing relatively cool gas.

Abstract: We selected 24 nearby late-type galaxies from the MUSE archive with kinematic coverage extending to at least one effective radius. Stellar kinematics were extracted with the GIST pipeline using pPXF, and axisymmetric Schwarzschild models were constructed with \textsc{dynamite}. The stellar orbits of each galaxy were classified by circularity into dynamically cold, warm, hot, and counter-rotating components, and we calculated the corresponding luminosity fractions of each component within $R_{\mathrm{e}}$. Our results confirm the mass-dependent trends in orbital structure previously identified from CALIFA galaxies and reproduced by IllustrisTNG. We further checked the morphology of each component and calculated the edge-on flattening of the cold, warm, and hot+CR components. With the high-quality MUSE data, we find that the dynamically warm components are common in low-mass late-type spiral galaxies, suggesting that their discs are dynamically warmer and vertically thicker than those of more massive spirals. We regard this as a tentative result, given the limited sample size.

Abstract: Context. Hickson Compact Groups (HCGs) present an ideal environment for studying galaxy interactions. HCG 16 is a group of five galaxies in an intermediate stage of evolution (Phase 2), with several members displaying signs of interaction, including distorted morphologies, tidal features, and enhanced star formation rates.

We examined the structure and dynamics of the H$α$ and HI lines in both 2D and 3D, and produced spatially resolved star formation histories to compare the inner and outer parts of the galaxies.

We identify a major star formation episode in HCG 16 beginning ~400-500 Myr ago, coinciding with the passage of NGC 848 close to the other member galaxies, and that continued at least until 50-60 Myr ago in some galaxies. The HI distribution is highly perturbed in the group, although HCG 16a retains a nearly regular disk, while HCG 16b has associated H$α$ tails.

We classify $212{,}704$ group-cluster infall pairs across $27{,}029$ clusters in the $(1\,h^{-1}\,\mathrm{Gpc})^3$ MDPL2 simulation into primary (cluster-connected), secondary (tributary), primary-outskirt, and isotropic channels using DisPerSE filaments. (ii) Within the primary class $M_\mathrm{flip}$ rises with the linear mass density of the host filament, from $12.96$ (thin tendrils) to $13.44$ (dense bridges); matching group mass, cluster mass and distance keeps the rise positive but leaves its size uncertain ($0.07-0.58$ dex). (iii) Isotropic groups fall below random alignment at $2\,R_{200}$ ($41$ per cent within $60^\circ$, a $4.3σ$ deficit). Primary groups show $85$ per cent velocity-filament alignment at $2\,R_{200}$, and isotropic groups infall $150-180\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$ faster, and more radially, out to $5\,R_{200}$. At matched distance isotropic groups are therefore predicted to reach orbital pericentres up to $10$ per cent smaller than primary groups, whose smaller pooled pericentres reflect only their proximity to the cluster. The topological route of accretion, and within it the host filament's linear density, shapes how groups enter clusters, testable with wide-area spectroscopic surveys.

Abstract: Disks surrounding active galactic nuclei contain many stars and black holes, which will inevitably encounter one another. We convolve our encounter model with a parametric family of natal stellar and binary populations, focused on varying binary fraction and period distribution. In our fiducial disk model, even though single-single interactions supply most encounters, binary-mediated interactions supply 68% of collisions; most encounters are well-approximated as parabolic; and stochastically-driven relative velocities are comparatively substantial and driven by gravitational perturbations from disk fluctuations and neighboring bodies. Our tools are ready to use in downstream applications, including several easily-expressed semianalytic approximations to key interaction phenomena.

Motivation: CGM是星系演化中吸积与外流的关键储库，但其热相金属丰度缺乏直接观测约束。传统方法依赖吸收线或发射线，难以覆盖大范围视线。FRB作为明亮的瞬时射电源，其DM对视线上的全部电离重子敏感，且可通过与金属丰度相关的氧吸收DM估计相结合，从而将DM分解出金属丰度信息，为直接测量CGM金属丰度提供新途径。

Method: 作者对25条FRB视线进行统计分析：将依赖金属丰度的氧吸收DM估计与不依赖金属丰度的FRB DM进行组合，从而直接约束银河系CGM热相的金属丰度。考虑IGM及介入晕中不同的弥散重子比例 fd = 0.0、0.6、0.8，并采用bootstrapping方法给出金属丰度的中位数及置信区间。

Result: 在 fd = 0.0、0.6、0.8 三种情形下，CGM金属丰度中位数分别为 Z_CGM = 0.12(+0.09/-0.04) Z☉、0.26(+0.18/-0.12) Z☉ 和 0.39(+0.26/-0.20) Z☉。fd = 0.8 时多条视线出现超太阳金属丰度，对银河系CGM而言不合理。即使采用保守下限（fd = 0.0），推断的CGM金属丰度仍显著高于IGM吸积预期（Z_IGM ≲ 0.1 Z☉）。同时观测到视线之间存在显著金属丰度差异。

Conclusion: 推断的CGM金属丰度高于IGM吸积预期，提供了有力经验证据表明大量金属增丰的星系反馈物质被高效保留在银河系CGM中。视线间显著的金属丰度变化指向一个高度各向异性的多相CGM，其结构由反馈外流与宇宙吸积之间的复杂相互作用塑造。该统计方法为利用FRB示踪CGM金属丰度开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Circumgalactic Medium (CGM) plays a critical role in galactic evolution, serving as the primary reservoir for accretion and outflows. In this work, we present a novel statistical analysis to derive CGM metallicity from the dispersion measure (DM) of 25 Fast Radio Burst (FRB) sightlines. By combining metallicity-dependent oxygen absorption DM estimates with metallicity-independent FRB DMs, we directly constrain the metallicity of the hot phase of the Milky Way (MW) CGM. Considering different diffuse baryon fractions ($f_{\text{d}} = 0.0, 0.6, 0.8$) in the Intergalactic Medium (IGM) and intervening halos, the median metallicities of CGM are $Z_{\text{CGM}} = 0.12_{-0.04}^{+0.09} \, Z_{\odot}$, $0.26_{-0.12}^{+0.18} \, Z_{\odot}$, and $0.39_{-0.20}^{+0.26} \, Z_{\odot}$, respectively, by the bootstrapping method. Super-solar metallicity is seen in multiple sightlines for $f_d=0.8$, which is implausible for MW CGM metallicities. Even under conservative lower-bound estimates ($f_{\text{d}} = 0.0$), the inferred CGM metallicity significantly exceeds expectations for IGM accretion ($Z_{\text{IGM}} \lesssim 0.1 \, Z_{\odot}$), providing strong empirical evidence that a large fraction of the metal-enriched galactic feedback material is efficiently retained within the Milky Way CGM. Furthermore, we observe significant sightline-to-sightline variation in inferred metallicities, pointing toward a highly anisotropic multi-phase CGM shaped by complex interactions between feedback outflows and cosmic accretion.

The spectrum shows a $\sim 5σ$ detection for the $\rm Hβ$-[OIII] complex and $\sim 4σ$ for $\rm Hα$ line, with fluxes of $3.2\pm0.9\times10^{-18}$ and $\rm 1.7\pm0.4\times10^{-18}\,erg\,s^{-1}\,cm^{-2}$, respectively. Based on JWST/NIRCam imaging, we find that GHZ8 consists of two components separated only by 0.16" ($\approx600$ pc): a bright, extended main component and a faint, compact southeastern clump, hereafter referred to as GHZ8-main and GHZ8-SE. Through morphological decomposition, we find demagnified $M_{UV}=-19.91\pm0.10$ and $-18.03\pm0.15$ mag for GHZ8-main and GHZ8-SE, with blue UV slopes of $β_{UV}=-2.53\pm0.22$ and $-3.45\pm0.49$, respectively, indicating that both components host young stellar populations. Combining the UV and $\rm Hα$ luminosities with the results from the spectro-photometric SED fitting, we constrain the star-formation activity of GHZ8-main, and find either a recent, short-lived burst of star formation that is now in its final phases, or continuous star formation over a relatively short period ($<50\,{\rm Myr}$). Globally, GHZ8 may be undergoing a merger-induced starburst, although deeper imaging or spatially resolved kinematic information is required for a more definitive interpretation.

A small fraction of these circumstellar discs also contain gas, detected in the form of emission lines from various ionic transitions (most commonly the near-infrared Ca ii triplet). All of the 21 known gas discs exhibit double-peaked line profile characteristic of a rotating flat disc. The mechanism behind the the generation of gas is still debated, but for at least one system it has been linked with the presence of a surviving planetesimal embedded within the dust disc. Abstract: Space weather is the study of the near-Earth impact of the processes on the Sun. In a this era of technology, space weather emerges as a crucial area of study wherein the state of the near-Earth conditions need to be modeled and forecasted. Crucially, the advent of machine learning provides us with opportunities to study this subject in a rather comprehensive manner. In this chapter, we treat the the processes arising from Sun to the impact on the ground as a set of coupled systems, which can be studied in isolation or in a coupled manner. We define these individual systems in terms of their state vector $\vec{S}$, which provides us with an opportunity to define the physical variables determining the system. This also allows us to consider the systems in terms of their observables, obtained through the action of operators as $\vec{O}[\vec{S}]$. In such a formalism, the machine learning algorithms serve as {\it learned operators}, defined generally as inference ($\mathcal{I}$), propagation ($\mathcal{P}$), and emulation ($\mathcal{E}$). Through such a formalism, we present how machine learning helps advance our understanding of the physical systems under study, while also accelerating the development of operational models for space weather forecasting.

We develop a Bayesian machine-learning framework that combines three probabilistic models through Bayesian hierarchical stacking (BHS): a Bayesian additive regression tree model, a two-process heteroscedastic sparse Gaussian process, and a hierarchical Bayesian neural network. The method propagates measurement uncertainties, accounts for correlations among inputs, and aggregates model variance into final predictive distributions. On a held-out test set the method attains a mean absolute relative difference (MARD) of 3.5% for mass and 2.1% for radius, improving on empirical-relation-based methods and standard machine-learning baselines. TL;DR: 利用全局模拟验证Tayler不稳定性的线性理论并确定其非线性饱和，表明Tayler-Spruit发电机只能通过磁地转波分支运行，且其稳态符合spruit2002dynamo提出的图像，排除了fuller2019slowing的小振幅饱和情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转恒星稳定分层区中磁不稳定性对发电机过程和角动量输运至关重要，绕卷环向场的Tayler不稳定性长期被认为是Tayler-Spruit发电机的驱动机制，但其非线性饱和行为认识不足，而这恰恰是发电机回路的核心环节。

Method: 采用全局数值模拟，验证新近完成的Tayler不稳定性线性理论，并确定其饱和行为；区分磁地转波与惯性波两支不稳定性，考察其增长时标、振荡频率、饱和幅度、环向场耗散时标以及非线性耦合产生轴对称极向场的能力。

Result: 证实磁地转波与惯性波均表现为过稳定性，二者以相近的磁地转时标增长但振荡频率不同；磁地转波以δB/B~1的大振幅饱和，并在磁地转时标上耗散环向场，而惯性波以δB/B≪1的小振幅饱和，在长得多的时标上侵蚀环向场；磁地转波还能通过非线性耦合高效再生轴对称极向场，惯性波则不能。

Variability classification was performed using Lomb-Scargle periodograms and iterative pre-whitening. For gravity-mode candidates, we searched for period-spacing patterns and fit them using the traditional approximation of rotation. We analysed the previously known $δ$ Sct star V705 Per, bringing the total sample to 149 targets. By simultaneously fitting the dipole and quadrupole period-spacing patterns, we estimate an asymptotic period spacing of $Π_0=4776\pm400$ s and a near-core rotation frequency of $f_{\mathrm{rot}}=1.317\pm0.05$ $\mathrm{d}^{-1}$. These results highlight the potential of asteroseismology to probe the internal structure and rotation of very young stars. The detected period-spacing pattern in HD 281229 shows that gravity-mode asteroseismology can provide meaningful constraints on the properties of stars at early evolutionary stages.

Abstract: White-light flares (WLFs) have long been considered rare on the Sun, as most magnetic reconnections occur in the corona, making it difficult to transport sufficient energy downward to heat the very low layers for WL continuum emission. Recently, we developed a new method to identify WLFs, which exhibits extraordinary detection capability. Combined with SDO/HMI's over 15 years of continuous photospheric observations, it provides us an unprecedented opportunity to address the long-standing debate about WLF ubiquity. A total of 1951 flares (547 B-class, 1316 C-class, and 88 M-class) from 769 low-activity active regions (ARs; flare number $<$ 10) observed during January 2011 - May 2025 are investigated. Among these, 1012 are identified as WLFs, giving an overall occurrence rate of 51.9\%. The occurrence rates for B-, C-, and M-class flares are 36.9\%, 56.9\%, and 85.2\%, respectively, showing a clear increase with flare class. The occurrence rate is also highly correlated with the AR's sunspot number. For the first time, we find that WL continuum radiation is widely present even among B-class flares. Different WLFs contain varying numbers of WL kernels, which exhibit distinct properties, evolutionary behaviors and different relationships with GOES soft X-ray flux and its time derivative. These results strongly suggest that WLFs are a common phenomenon on the Sun.

Abstract: The population of extremely long-period Mira variables in the Milky Way remains poorly characterized. Their rarity and heavy circumstellar obscuration have precluded systematic studies in optical surveys, yet they are expected to trace the most massive stars evolving through the Asymptotic Giant Branch (AGB). We present IRTF/SpeX spectroscopy of the longest-period ($P\gtrsim1000$ d) Galactic Mira variables identified with the Palomar Gattini-IR survey. Twelve of thirteen targets exhibit characteristics consistent with massive O-rich AGB stars. In contrast, one peculiar source IRAS 19552+3142 exhibits i) a large-amplitude ($ΔJ\gtrsim2$ mag) pulsations with $P\approx1060$ d, ii) strong C$_2$ and CO molecular bands indicating C/O$\gtrsim1$, and iii) SiC emission at $\approx11.3\,μ$m in archival mid-infrared spectra. Its spectroscopically confirmed chemistry makes it the longest period C-rich Galactic Mira variable known; together with the rarity of similar objects, this source is difficult to reconcile with standard massive AGB evolution where hot-bottom burning maintains an O-rich surface composition. Instead, its properties may be explained by super-AGB evolution where dredge-out episodes produce a C-rich surface and circumstellar material. Its high luminosity ($M_{[4.5]}\lesssim-10$ mag), red color ($[3.6] - [4.5]\gtrsim2.0$ mag), and high mass-loss rate ($\gtrsim 10^{-4} \mathrm{M_\odot yr^{-1}}$) resemble extreme AGB star progenitors of intermediate-luminosity red transients in nearby galaxies and a known chemically peculiar super-AGB star candidate in the Small Magellanic Cloud. IRAS 19552+3142 may thus provide a rare look at a Galactic super-AGB star in the thermally pulsing phase. Our results motivate improved theoretical predictions and long-baseline infrared searches for these short-lived, dust-enshrouded endpoints of stellar evolution.

Abstract: Context. Pulsating white dwarfs, such as ZZ Ceti stars, allow for the study of white dwarf rotation through rotationally split pulsation modes. Space-based photometry has also revealed outbursts in some cool ZZ Ceti stars.

Candidate rotationally split multiplets were identified automatically and visually validated. Outburst candidates were selected from smoothed flux excursions, followed by light curve inspection and pixel-level TESS diagnostics.
  Results. We identify 11 ZZ Ceti stars with accepted triplet candidates and derive rotation periods of about 8-94 h; three triplet detections are reported here for the first time. The targets are broadly consistent with the dispersion of rotation periods found in previous studies. The time-domain search produced 19 outburst-like events in 11 stars after light curve inspection. Pixel-level diagnostics indicate that most are likely caused by foreground asteroid contamination. We identify three stars (TIC 219442838, TIC 307908863, and TIC 630426257) as candidate stellar outbursters. None belong to the accepted triplet-rotation sample.

Abstract: The Southern Galactic Disk Survey (GDS) monitored a mosaic of 268 fields along a $6^\circ$-wide stripe in the southern galactic disk from September 2010 to September 2019. The comparison with the cross-match catalogue of Gaia DR3 sources with variables from the literature compiled by Gavras et al. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 最优模型成功再现了星云的主要形态与运动学特征。确认赤道破碎环相对天空平面倾斜约60°，而双极外流相对视线的倾角为46°，两者存在轻微错位。

Abstract: We present a morpho-kinematic study of the Necklace Nebula based on spatially resolved spectroscopic observations and 3D modeling performed with the SHAPE software. The nebula is characterized by an equatorial disrupted ring accompanied by bipolar outflows. The geometry and velocity field of these different components are derived from the comparison between observed and synthetic images and position-velocity diagrams. The best model successfully reproduces the main morphological and kinematic features of the nebula, allowing us to constrain its 3D structure. We confirm that the disrupted ring is tilted about 60° from the plane of the sky, but the bipolar outflows are inclined by 46° with respect to the line of sight. This situation might indicate that the bipolar structures are not simple extensions of the equatorial ring, but instead correspond to a distinct mass-ejection event. The slight misalignment between these two components suggests either precession of the ejection axis, a temporal change in the dominant mass-loss geometry, or a misalignment in the magnetic field. Given that the Necklace Nebula hosts a close binary central star and is a well-established post-common envelope planetary nebula, our results support a scenario in which binary interaction plays a central role in shaping its morphology, even before the common envelope phase.

Result: 两类分类均显示出系统性的振荡差异：SHAPE聚类分离出与高频功率增强相关的谱线形态，部分情况下4–6 mHz波段的振荡超过光球p模波段；ABS聚类则显示高频振荡的相对贡献随发射强度和磁通量增加而单调上升。

Abstract: Waves in solar magnetic elements provide a pathway for transporting and dissipating energy in the chromosphere, yet the relationship between photospheric oscillations and chromospheric spectral structure remains incompletely understood. We investigate whether distinct chromospheric Mg II spectral states are linked to different populations of photospheric kink mode oscillations, with particular focus on the role of high frequency (~5mHz) waves. Magnetic elements are tracked in SDO/HMI magnetograms to derive horizontal velocity power spectra, while corresponding Mg II h + k profiles from IRIS are classified using k-means clustering into morphology-based (SHAPE) and intensity-based (ABS) groups. Both classifications exhibit systematic differences in oscillatory behavior. The SHAPE clusters isolate spectral morphologies associated with enhanced high-frequency power, including cases where oscillations in the 4-6 mHz band exceed those in the photospheric p-mode band. In contrast, the ABS clusters show a monotonic increase in the relative contribution of high-frequency oscillations with increasing emission strength and magnetic flux. These results indicate that high-frequency oscillations are both selectively enhanced in specific chromospheric states and systematically strengthened in stronger magnetic environments. This suggests that magnetic structuring plays a key role in regulating both the transmission and the conversion of kink mode wave energy into observable chromospheric signatures.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Stellar abundances are generated by complex chemical enrichment pathways yet show strong correlation structure. We test how well high-quality spectra of low-alpha disk stars can be reconstructed using low-dimensional models. By validating model performance in flux space, we quantify and locate where residual information persists. Using HARPS spectra (R = 115,000, SNR > 100) and labels derived with Korg, we train generative models (The Cannon) conditioned on stellar parameters Teff, log(g), [M/H], vsini, and vmic, and three abundance labels, [Ti/H], [Y/H], and [Nd/H], representing distinct nucleosynthetic families. Evaluating model performance on unseen test stars through flux residuals, the model reproduces the global spectra to a median residual amplitude of 0.3%. Deviations in 15 absorption features for unlabelled elements are typically < 0.7%. Residuals of ~1% correspond to abundance differences of 0.01-0.05 dex. Degrading the spectra to medium resolution (R = 28,000) decreases the median residual amplitude from 0.3% to 0.2%, redistributing the residual signal over the instrumental line profile. Our publicly available model reproduces features from 29 elements to sub-percent precision across -1.13 < [M/H] < 0.29. This demonstrates that the dominant chemical variation encoded in low-alpha disk spectra can be captured by a low-dimensional model, while weaker chemical information remains encoded in flux residuals. High-resolution, high-SNR spectra (R ~ 100,000, SNR > 100) are essential to resolve the hierarchical layers of chemical information beyond this low-dimensional structure. The model provides a benchmark for assessing the chemical information accessible to current surveys like 4MOST and GALAH 2, as well as upcoming facilities like VLT/HRMOS and GMT/G-CLEF.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳大气中的喷流普遍存在，对质量输运、大气加热和能量供应有重要作用，但其触发机制仍不清楚；为揭示喷流演化的精细过程，需要高分辨率、全过程的观测剖析。

Result: 清晰识别出喷流的双阶段运动学特征，即由交换式重联阶段演变为耀斑重联阶段；该双阶段喷流在磁场形态、Hα轮廓、喷流速度、喷流高度、X射线通量、环顶源及温度等方面均表现出彼此区分的双阶段特征，并可被一个耦合交换式重联与耀斑重联的物理图像自然统一。

Conclusion: 喷流的触发与演化可由交换式重联与耀斑重联的耦合过程解释，这为理解磁流绳（filament）驱动的喷流物理过程提供了新见解。

Abstract: Jets are ubiquitous eruptive activities in the solar atmosphere, playing an important role in mass transport, atmospheric heating, and energy supply. However, the triggering mechanism remains elusive. To reveal the fine process of jet evolution, we study a jet associated with a B-class flare that involves in two distinct stages. Analyzing the complete evolution of a solar jet captured by the Solar Dynamics Observatory (SDO), we clearly identify its dual-stage kinematics, evolving from interchange to flare reconnection. This dual-stage jet is characterized by distinct dual-stage signatures in magnetic morphology, H$α$ profile, jet speed, jet height, X-ray flux, loop-top source, and temperature. These differences are naturally unified by a physical picture coupling interchange and flare reconnection, offering new insights into understanding of the physical process of filament-induced jets.

</details>

*S. W. Zhou,W. Su,S. Y. Zhou,C. F. Li,J. X Zhang*

Result: 线性本征模分析实现了太阳上不同族惯性模式的识别与分类，并揭示其对深部对流区弱约束参数的强诊断潜力；线性稳定模式的振幅可由随机受迫响应估算，不稳定模式需非线性建模确定；大振幅惯性模式可能在太阳内部的熱量与角动量输运中扮演动力学角色。

Abstract: Inertial modes are low-frequency global oscillations in rotating fluids, restored by the Coriolis force. Long-term observations of horizontal flows on the solar surface have led to the detection of various inertial modes on the Sun, opening a new window into the structure and dynamics of the solar interior. Here, we focus on recent advances in the theoretical modeling of solar inertial modes within a purely hydrodynamic framework, leaving magnetohydrodynamic effects beyond the scope of this review. Because the low-frequency oscillations of a differentially rotating spherical shell depend sensitively on the rotation profile and the properties of the medium, they are primarily studied using numerical models. We first summarize how linear eigenmode analyses have enabled the identification and classification of different families of inertial modes observed on the Sun, and how these models reveal the strong diagnostic potential of inertial modes for probing poorly constrained parameters in the deep convection zone, such as turbulent viscosity and superadiabaticity. The amplitudes of linearly stable modes can be estimated from their response to stochastic forcing, whereas those of linearly unstable modes must be determined using nonlinear numerical modeling. We further discuss the possible dynamical roles of large-amplitude inertial modes in transporting heat and angular momentum in the Sun. By clarifying what has been established and what remains uncertain, this review provides a framework for interpreting current observations and guiding future theoretical developments in this rapidly evolving field.

Additionally, we identify 3 $r$+$s$ stars whose abundance patterns reveal combined contributions from both the $r$- and $s$-processes. We further propose a modified diagnostic scheme for the identification of CEMP-$r$+$s$ stars, defined as: -0.2 $\leqslant$ $\mathrm{[La/Nd]}$ $\leqslant$ 0.2 and -0.5 $\leqslant$ $\mathrm{[Eu/Nd]}$ $\leqslant$ 0.0.

The remaining system, ZTF J2116+2446, shows only hydrogen lines, making it the shortest-period UCB with hydrogen but no detected helium; it may have evolved from a white dwarf-brown dwarf binary. The nine new systems increase the number of published eclipsing accreting UCBs from 21 to 30. Because they are eclipsing, they are immediately available for the high-speed photometric modelling that yields model-independent masses and radii, to be presented in future work, including for the three hydrogen-rich systems that are key tests of the evolved cataclysmic-variable channel.

Motivation: 经典Be星以接近临界速度的比例高速自转，并被赤道星周盘环绕。快速自转导致恒星扁率与引力昏暗，使光谱同时依赖恒星参数和倾角；因此需要检验现有光谱方法（ZPEKTR）在不同自转速率下反演倾角的可信度，并弥补干涉测量样本有限的不足。

Conclusion: ZPEKTR为约束快速自转大质量星的倾角与自转性质提供了有前景的光谱方法；通过考虑恒星形变与引力昏暗，它可作为干涉观测的实用替代手段，并支持对更大样本经典Be星的分析。

Abstract: Classical Be stars are non-supergiant B-type stars that rotate at a significant fraction of their critical velocity and are surrounded by equatorial circumstellar discs. Rapid rotation induces stellar oblateness and gravity darkening, making the emergent spectrum dependent on both the stellar parameters and the inclination angle. We assess the reliability of the ZPEKTR code for determining inclination angles across different rotation rates, identify the minimum rotation rate required for reliable estimates, and extend our previous analysis to derive consistent stellar parameters for 21 classical Be stars. We evaluated the method through noise-injection tests on synthetic spectra spanning a broad range of rotational rates and inclination angles. We constructed a grid of gravity-darkened synthetic spectra with ZPEKTR, covering the atmospheric and rotational parameter space of classical Be stars. Stellar and rotational parameters were derived through Q minimisation of the HeI 4388, 4471, and 6678 $Å$ line profiles. We applied the method to 21 classical Be stars using archival BeSOS/PUCHEROS spectra and new observations from the LCO/NRES network. The rotation rate was reliably recovered across the tested inclinations, while inclination estimates improved with increasing rotation rate. Reliable inclination estimates were obtained for V/Vc $\geq$ 0.46. For the observed sample, the derived inclination angles show excellent agreement with independent interferometric measurements, yielding $R^{2}$ = 0.985. ZPEKTR provides a promising spectroscopic method for constraining inclination angles and rotational properties in rapidly rotating massive stars. By accounting for stellar deformation and gravity darkening, it offers a practical alternative to interferometric observations and enables the analysis of larger samples of classical Be stars.

Abstract: We present an asteroseismic survey of 1,526,218 A- and F-type stars in and around the $δ$ Scuti instability strip using TESS 10-minute cadence data, which resolve the full $δ$ Scuti pulsation spectra across nearly the entire sky. We derive homogeneous stellar parameters from Gaia DR3 parallaxes and archival photometry, with temperatures that agree with interferometry to within ~300 K. A Positive--Unlabelled classifier identifies 156,706 $δ$ Scuti pulsators, which on average rotate significantly faster than non-variable A-type stars. Within the instability strip in the $T_{eff}$--$\log L$ plane, the pulsator fraction peaks at 30% over the full sample and rises to 80% in the brightest magnitude bins. Detection sensitivity therefore accounts for much, but not all, of the apparent impurity of the strip. Our period--luminosity relation agrees with previous determinations, and we recast it as a scale-invariant period--density relation that connects directly to the pulsation constant. We find that the secondary ridge in these relations is probably a combination of several overtone modes. The $δ$ Scuti stars closest to the fundamental ridge have systematically lower rotational velocities and occupy a distinct region of the instability strip. Finally, a cross-match with a Gaia catalog of open clusters reveals a correlation between the mean pulsation frequency of $δ$ Scuti stars and cluster age, suggesting a new ensemble asteroseismic age indicator for young stellar populations.

<details>

Motivation: 近距小行星的双峰半径分布对其形成与演化给出重要约束，光致蒸发与核心供能质量损失两种机制被广泛用于解释该分布，但两模型的物理假设及核心光度的实际作用尚未被系统审视。

Method: 建立基于 SEAMIST 演化模型的群体合成框架，自洽耦合两种质量损失机制，并引入包层金属丰度作为变量，量化核心光度在沸腾（boil-off）与光致蒸发阶段对包层再平衡热能的贡献及对半径分布的影响。

Result: 包层金属丰度强烈影响半径分布，50-100 倍太阳金属丰度与观测匹配良好；沸腾过程主导大气质量损失，光致蒸发至多移除包层质量的 3 wt%；核心光度仅提供沸腾期包层再平衡可用热能的约 10-20%，绝对质量损失增强仅约 0.4 wt%，在光致蒸发期效应更大但对长期半径分布影响甚微；最佳拟合模型中辐射大气贡献约 20% 的总光学半径。

Conclusion: 核心供能质量损失更适合被理解为核心光度通过改变下边界条件而对大气逃逸产生的修正，而非独立的质量损失机制；未来需改进亚海王星演化模型中的辐射大气建模，以更准确理解观测到的半径分布。

Abstract: The observed bimodal radius distribution of close-in small planets provides important constraints on their formation and evolution. Two atmospheric escape mechanisms, photoevaporation and core-powered mass loss, have been widely invoked to explain this distribution. In this work, we revisit the physical assumptions underlying these models and investigate the role of core luminosity in driving outflows and shaping the radius distribution. We develop a population synthesis framework based on the SEAMIST evolution model, which self-consistently couples the two mass-loss mechanisms while accounting for envelope metallicity. We find that envelope metallicity strongly affects the resulting radius distribution, with 50-100$\times$ solar metallicity providing a good match to the observed population. Boil-off generally dominates atmospheric mass loss, while photoevaporation typically removes at most 3 wt\% of the envelope mass. We find that core luminosity contributes only $\sim$10-20\% of the thermal energy available for envelope re-equilibration during boil-off, producing an absolute mass-loss enhancement of only $\sim$0.4wt\%. Its effect is larger during photoevaporation, where the longer mass-loss timescale allows core heating to accumulate. Nevertheless, core luminosity has little impact on the long-term planetary radius distribution. These results suggest that core-powered mass loss is better understood as a modification to atmospheric escape through its lower-boundary conditions rather than as a distinct mass-loss mechanism. In our best-fit model, the radiative atmosphere contributes a significant $\sim$20\% of the total optical radius. Consequently, we highlight the need for improved radiative-atmosphere modeling in sub-Neptune evolution models to better understand the observed population distribution.

</details>

Abstract: The young intermediate-mass star AB Aurigae is surrounded by a transition disk whose cavity contains several proposed protoplanets, including the debated source "AB Aur b" at a projected separation of $\approx 0.6$ arcsec ($\approx 93$ au). To search for circumplanetary material associated with these candidates, we have obtained the most sensitive ($\sim 20$ $μ$Jy/beam rms) and highest angular resolution ($\sim 30$ mas) ALMA Band 7 (0.88 mm) observations of AB Aur to date. We recover the asymmetric dust ring in the continuum emission peaking at $\sim 0.9$ arcsec ($\sim 140$ au) and compact emission at the stellar position, but detect no point source at the location of AB Aur b or any of the other proposed protoplanet candidates down to a $3σ$ limit of $60$ $μ$Jy. For optically thin emission, this corresponds to a dust mass of $\lesssim 0.0059 M_\oplus$ (for a dust temperature of 40 K), comparable to the circumplanetary disk around PDS 70 c. The $^{12}$CO emission fills the continuum cavity, revealing inner spiral structure and a clear rotation pattern. The gas at the position of AB Aur b is smoothly connected to the ambient disk with no localized excess or obvious kinematic deviation. If any proposed candidates are accreting, their circumplanetary material must have a low dust mass, a small physical size, dust that is optically thick, or a dearth of large mm-emitting grains.

Extending our previous work, we insert three planets with planet-to-star mass ratio \(q = 0.01\) (\(\sim 10\) Jupiter masses) at 0.45, 0.9, and 1.8 times the magnetospheric truncation radius, \(R_T\) , thereby probing the low-density cavity, disk-cavity boundary, and turbulent inner disk. The middle planet, located near \(R_T\), develops a persistent polar CPD whose angular-momentum vector is parallel to the disk midplane, demonstrating that coherent circumplanetary structures can form within highly dynamic magnetospheric flows. Based on both the effective Bondi radius and thermal-mass criterion, we identify conditions under which massive giant planets form CPDs in the inner disk. Estimated planetary inflow accretion rates imply mass-doubling timescales \(\gtrsim 10\) Myr due to the low disk surface density, suggesting that, in the magnetically active zone, these planets undergo only limited late-stage growth before disk dispersal.

Abstract: The NSF-DOE Vera C. Rubin Observatory's combination of survey speed, sky coverage, and depth provides a unique opportunity to study the distant Trans-Neptunian region, including the Neptune Trojans. The Neptune Trojans are small bodies on orbits with Neptune's semimajor axis that are locked in the 1:1 mean motion resonance librating about the ice giant's stable Lagrange points, L4 (leading) and L5 (trailing), located 60$^{\circ}$ ahead of or behind the planet. For more than 15 years, the on-sky location of the L5 Neptune Trojan cloud has been coincident with the Galactic plane and Galactic center; the high stellar density has significantly hindered the discovery of L5 Neptune Trojans. In 2025, the L5 cloud has now moved sufficiently out of the galactic plane to overlap with the sky covered by Rubin Observatory's Science Validation (SV) survey that was executed during the Observatory's commissioning phase. We report on two new confirmed long-term stable (primordial) members of the Neptune L5 Trojan cloud (2025 MH348 and 2025 NN80), doubling the known sample, and one candidate (2025 MD138) that have been found in a preliminary search of the SV survey observations. We present our study to characterize the physical and dynamical properties of these objects and compare the L5 sample to the well-studied L4 cloud. This work also presents the first characterization of objects discovered by Rubin Observatory's moving object discovery pipelines using observations taken in an LSST-like cadence and as such, provides a blueprint for LSST-era analyses.

Incorporating hydrogen-silicate miscibility fundamentally alters atmospheric escape compared to chemically-inert models. This results from an increased envelope mean molecular weight, interior hydrogen sequestration, and modified compositional gradients in miscible planets. Miscible planets sequester hydrogen in their interiors post-impact, reducing the hydrogen available in the envelope for mass loss. As planets cool, hydrogen exsolves into their envelopes. Because more massive planets cool slower, they sequester hydrogen for longer, undergoing less mass loss. Abstract: Gravity affects the passage of time, as described by General Relativity. On Earth, the rate of a clock as seen by a fixed observer changes by about one part in $10^{16}$ per metre in height. Experimental demonstrations have to date focused primarily on static potential differences. Yet, the potentials of the Moon, the Sun and other celestial bodies as well as the Earth's response to their tidal forcing change clock rates by several $10^{-17}$, providing a predictable test signal to demonstrate optical clock networks resolving small variations in relativistic time dilation. Here, we report chronometric observations of time-varying gravity potential differences using a network of optical clocks and phase-stabilized fibre links in Europe. The measured rate shifts track the tidal variations, resolving the predicted signal within 1 d of measurement and agreeing with model predictions. These results extend experimental chronometric levelling from static to dynamic potential differences and highlight the need to account for the time dependence of relativistic time dilation in frequency metrology. They also open the prospect for quantum clock networks with improved stability and availability to move beyond known tides and monitor other gravity potential variations, such as those arising from surface deformation and mass redistribution in geologically or volcanically active regions.
