Radar diameter (4.9 km) and H_V≈16.4 yield low albedo p_V~0.02; ARCTIC photometry gives g-r=0.49, r-i=0.20, i-z=0.02, a*=-0.05, slope 3.4%/100 nm, favoring an X-type with Tholen P-type interpretation. NEOMOD3 predicts 66% inner Main Belt delivery and favors intermediate albedo, while Atira is an uncommon low-albedo outcome.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Characterizing inner-Earth asteroids (IEOs) is observationally difficult, so it is important to test whether current population/delivery models (NEOMOD3) can reproduce the compositional diversity seen in this poorly sampled population.

Method: Radar-derived diameter (4.9 km) and absolute magnitude H_V≈16.4 are used to compute geometric albedo. Photometry with the ARC 3.5-m telescope and ARCTIC yields g-r, r-i, i-z colors, a*, and g-to-i spectral slope. These are compared with NEOMOD3 delivery-channel and albedo-category probability predictions, and extended to 38 IEOs (including Atira and 'Ayló'chaxnim).

Result: Atira has p_V~0.02, g-r=0.49±0.03, r-i=0.20±0.02, i-z=0.02±0.03, a*=-0.05±0.03, slope 3.4±0.9%/100 nm; provisional X-type, with low albedo supporting Tholen P-type. NEOMOD3 gives ~66% inner Main Belt delivery and 26% central/outer Main Belt; albedo-category probabilities 0.07±0.02 (p_V<0.1), 0.64±0.04 (0.1≤p_V<0.3), 0.29±0.04 (p_V≥0.3). Across 38 IEOs, inner Main Belt delivery generally dominates and intermediate albedo is favored for 33 objects.

Conclusion: Atira is an uncommon low-albedo outcome, compatible with delivery of dark material from the inner Main Belt or more distant primitive populations. Further IEO characterization will test whether current models reproduce their compositional diversity.

Abstract: We compare the physical properties of inner-Earth asteroid (163693) Atira with the NEOMOD3 population model. Its radar-derived diameter of 4.9 km and absolute magnitude H$_V\simeq$16.4 imply a geometric albedo of p$_V\sim$0.02. Photometry with the Astrophysical Research Consortium 3.5-m telescope and ARCTIC yields g-r=0.49$\pm$0.03, r-i=0.20$\pm$0.02, i-z=0.02$\pm$0.03, a$^\ast$=$-0.05\pm0.03$, and a g-to-i spectral slope of 3.4$\pm$0.9$\%$/100 nm. The photometric reflectance favors a provisional X-type classification; combined with the low albedo, it supports a Tholen P-type interpretation. NEOMOD3 assigns an approximately 66$\%$ probability of delivery through inner Main Belt channels and 26$\%$ to central and outer Main Belt channels. Atira's NEOMOD3 albedo-category probabilities are $0.07\pm0.02$, $0.64\pm0.04$, and $0.29\pm0.04$ for p$_V<$0.1, 0.1$\leq$p$_V<$0.3, and p$_V\geq$0.3, respectively. Across 38 inner-Earth objects, including Atira and 'Ayló'chaxnim, inner Main Belt delivery generally dominates, and intermediate albedo is favored for 33 objects. Atira therefore represents an uncommon low-albedo outcome, compatible with delivery of dark material from the inner Main Belt or more distant primitive populations. Further characterization of inner-Earth asteroids will test whether current population models reproduce their compositional diversity.

</details>


### [86] [TEMPEST - A modular Python-based thermophysical model for rough, irregularly-shaped airless bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40406)
*D. G. Lyster,C. J. A. Howett*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: TEMPEST是一个模块化、开源的Python日周期热演化模型，用于不规则无大气天体表面温度模拟，支持任意BRDF多次散射与亚面元尺度粗糙度，经验证与现有模型及LRO实测数据吻合，并已应用于多任务数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较无大气天体表面温度的观测值与预测值，对解释表面过程、约束活动性和反演表面性质至关重要。现有模型在非朗伯多次散射、亚面元粗糙度处理、计算效率和通用性方面存在不足，需要一个可灵活配置且高效的公开工具。

Method: TEMPEST采用模块化Python架构：（1）以任意BRDF复现非朗伯多次散射，并通过代表性球截面几何引入亚面元尺度粗糙度；（2）提供显式时间步进一维周期导热求解器（受Spencer的Thermprojrs启发）与半隐式Crank-Nicolson求解器，且可与其他热求解器耦合；（3）利用预计算查找表快速应用朗伯、Lommel-Seeliger、Hapke或经验BRDF数据，便于评估永久阴影区与部分阴影地形中的散射效应；（4）并行化设计，可在个人计算机上数分钟内完成数千面元计算，并可扩展至十万面元以上的高性能服务器；验证分别针对Thermprojrs（一维）、Sorli二元热物理模型（三维）及LRO经验热数据进行。

Result: 三项验证均显示良好一致性。应用于25143 Itokawa高分辨率形状模型表明，自阴影与辐射互加热对局部温度控制具有重要作用。该工具已被用于分析Lucy任务、Galileo及地面观测数据，并可适配Comet Interceptor和Europa Clipper等任务即将获得的数据集。

Conclusion: TEMPEST为不规则天体表面温度建模提供了一个准确、可扩展、开源且通用的工具，兼顾物理完备性与计算效率，适用于当前与未来多目标探测任务的数据分析。

Abstract: Comparing observed and predicted surface temperatures on airless planetary bodies is crucial for interpreting their surface processes, constraining activity, and deriving their surface properties. We present a new tool in this endeavour: TEMPEST (Thermophysical Evolution Model for Planetary Environment Surface Temperatures). It is a modular, open-source Python model for diurnal thermal evolution on irregular bodies. TEMPEST can reproduce non-Lambertian multiple scattering using arbitrary Bidirectional Reflectance Distribution Functions (BRDFs) and notably incorporates surface roughness on sub-facet scales through representative sphere-section geometry. The model includes an explicit time-stepping one-dimensional periodic conduction solver influenced by Spencer's thermal model Thermprojrs, and a semi-implicit Crank-Nicolson solver, and can be paired with other thermal solvers. Precomputed look-up tables enable fast application of scattering laws (Lambertian, Lommel-Seeliger, Hapke, or input empirical BRDF data), allowing the user to assess their effects in permanently shadowed regions or partially shadowed terrains. TEMPEST is parallelised, running on personal computers in minutes for thousands of facets and scaling to high-performance servers for models exceeding 100,000 facets. Validation was performed against Thermprojrs in one dimension, against Sorli's Binary Thermophysical Model in three dimensions, and with empirical thermal data from the Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter, showing good agreement in each case. Application to the high-resolution shape-model of 25143 Itokawa shows the importance of self-shadowing and radiative mutual heating in controlling local temperatures. TEMPEST has already been used to analyse data from the Lucy Mission, Galileo, and ground-based observations, and is adaptable to upcoming datasets from targets of missions such as Comet Interceptor and Europa Clipper.

</details>


### [87] [Mapping Stellar Obliquity Across Space and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40527)
*Lauren I. Biddle,Brendan P. Bowler*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 通过比较114个单星系统的恒星倾角与轨道倾角，发现原行星盘、碎片盘、暖木星与热木星四类样本的最小错位率在1σ内一致（20%-33%），说明类太阳恒星的自转轴倾角大多在形成时即已确立，后续空间与时间演化有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星倾角（恒星自转角动量与行星轨道角动量之间的夹角）记录了行星系统的初始条件与动力学历史。倾角随轨道距离的变化可能源于原初盘结构（如内外区错位的断裂或扭曲原行星盘），也可能源于与行星或外部摄动者的后期动力学相互作用。因此，跨轨道距离比较倾角可探测这些过程的空间印记，跨年龄比较则可约束其时间演化。作者希望同时在空间与时间两个维度检验倾角演化的迹象。

Method: 选取114颗单颗类太阳恒星，划分为四个覆盖不同轨道距离与年龄区间的样本：49个原行星盘系统、24个碎片盘系统、21个暖木星系统、20个热木星系统；将恒星倾角（自转轴倾角）与行星轨道倾角进行比较，并统计各样本的最小错位率及最小倾角分布。

Result: 最小错位率分别为：原行星盘系统33^{+7}_{-6}%、碎片盘系统21^{+9}_{-7}%、暖木星系统23^{+10}_{-8}%、热木星系统20^{+9}_{-8}%。四类样本的比率在1σ内相互一致；最小倾角分布随轨道距离或系统年龄均无显著变化。

Conclusion: 类太阳恒星的恒星倾角很可能在形成时即基本确立，后续在空间与时间上的演化均有限。这一结果与"形成后倾角演化稀少、仅限小角度错位、或局限于本文未考察的参数空间区域（如存在近距恒星伴星、热的主星、小行星、长周期巨行星的系统）"等情形相容。

Abstract: The angle between a star's rotational angular momentum vector and the orbital angular momentum vector of its planets---stellar obliquity---encodes the initial conditions and dynamical history of planetary systems. Variations in obliquity with orbital separation may arise from either primordial disk structure (e.g., broken or warped protoplanetary disks with misaligned inner and outer regions) or subsequent dynamical interactions with planets or external perturbers. Comparing obliquities across orbital distance therefore probes the spatial imprint of these processes, while comparisons across age constrain their temporal evolution. To test for signatures of obliquity evolution in both space and time, we compare stellar inclinations with orbital inclinations for 114 single Sun-like stars spanning four samples that probe distinct regimes of orbital separation and age: 49 protoplanetary disk systems, 24 debris disk systems, 21 warm Jupiter systems, and 20 hot Jupiter systems. We find minimum misalignment rates of $33^{+7}_{-6}$% for protoplanetary disk systems, $21^{+9}_{-7}$% for debris disk systems, $23^{+10}_{-8}$% for warm Jupiter systems, and $20^{+9}_{-8}$% for hot Jupiter systems. These rates are consistent within 1$σ$ across all samples. The absence of significant variation in minimum-obliquity distributions with either orbital separation or system age suggests that stellar obliquities for Sun-like stars may be largely established at formation with limited subsequent evolution in both space and time. These results are consistent with post-formation inclination evolution being either infrequent, confined to small misalignments, or restricted to regions of parameter space not examined here---such as systems with close stellar companions, hot host stars, small planets, and long-period giant planets.

</details>

### [88] [Identifying Scientifically Interesting Galaxies with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37747)
*Yixuan Cheng,Nolan Koblischke,Jo Bovy*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 利用现成视觉语言模型（Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite）直接从自然语言指令评估星系图像的科学价值，在2万张哈勃图像上将98.2%的参考异常排进前约2000名，成本约每万张5.05美元，并新发布102个高候选天体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天文图像中的异常搜索旨在发现值得后续观测的稀有天体，但统计上最不寻常的目标未必最具科学价值；传统方法依赖标注数据和机器学习流程，难以直接对接"科学兴趣"这一模糊目标。

Method: 构建含167个经机器学习与专家核验的参考异常的2万张哈勃空间望远镜图像样本；用预训练视觉语言模型按自然语言指令对图像打"科学兴趣"分并排序，无需在目标数据上训练；统计参考异常在排序中的命中率并估算推理成本，同时对高分候选进行人工检查。

Result: Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite 将98.2%的参考异常纳入最高排名的约2000张图像中，推理成本约每万张5.05美元；成功复现已知引力透镜、水母星系候选和带喷流的星系，并公开102个高分候选供进一步研究。

Conclusion: 无需在目标数据上训练的现成视觉语言模型，可作为按科学相关性而非稀有性对大规模图像数据集排序的实用工具，为天文异常筛查提供了低成本、零样本的新范式。

Abstract: Anomaly searches in large astronomical image datasets aim to identify rare objects for follow-up investigation. Yet the objects that are most statistically unusual are not necessarily those of greatest scientific relevance. We investigate whether pretrained vision-language models can directly assess scientific interest from natural-language instructions, enabling galaxy images to be ranked without labeled examples. We evaluate models on a 20,000-image Hubble Space Telescope sample that includes 167 reference anomalies previously identified through machine learning and expert inspection. By scoring images for scientific interest, Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite places 98.2% of the reference anomalies within the highest-ranked ~2,000 images, at a present-day inference cost of approximately $5.05 per 10,000 images. We recover known gravitational lenses, jellyfish-galaxy candidates, and galaxies with visible jets, and release 102 high-scoring candidates for further investigation. These results show that an off-the-shelf vision-language model, without any training on the target data, offers a practical way to rank large image datasets by scientific relevance rather than rarity. The project code is available at https://github.com/TheCheng2005/Galaxy-Anomaly-Search/

</details>


### [89] [NORI: a GPU-accelerated code for simulating self-gravitating and self-interacting dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37768)
*Oliver Zier,Ruediger Pakmor,Xuejian Shen,Rongrong Liu,Lucas Schleuss,Mark Vogelsberger,Lars Hernquist*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: NORI是一个可在CPU和GPU上运行的N体代码，用蒙特卡洛方法模拟孤立自引力系统的弹性各向同性自相互作用，通过数据复制避免MPI通信，在核心坍缩模拟中比AREPO-2快约30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）可形成暗物质晕核心并随后发生引力热坍缩，使中心密度远超NFW晕；模拟该坍缩计算量大，需要极短时间步和大量粒子来分辨收缩的核心，因此需要更高效的计算工具。

Method: 提出NORI代码：支持CPU与GPU的N体代码，采用蒙特卡洛方法模拟弹性、各向同性自相互作用。为避免树遍历中的MPI通信，CPU模式下每个计算节点、GPU模式下每个GPU或GPU组都保存全部粒子的副本及各自按Morton序构建的树；采用固定求值顺序使CPU与GPU结果逐位一致；按顺序施加散射事件使每对粒子的动量和能量守恒到舍入误差，并允许每个粒子每步多次散射；两种代码均使用全局时间步。

Result: CPU版本比AREPO-2实现快数倍；单个4块A100 GPU节点每时间步比112核CPU节点上的AREPO-2快最多60倍；完整核心坍缩模拟（AREPO-2使用个体时间步）中，中心密度达到NFW特征密度ρ_s的10³–10⁴倍时加速约30倍。跟踪了r₂₀₀内10⁸个粒子的晕至中心密度10⁴ρ_s。受数据复制限制，粒子数上限约2×10⁹。

Conclusion: NORI为孤立SIDM模拟（包括引力热坍缩和星系团碰撞）提供了高效工具，在约2×10⁹粒子限制内适用。

Abstract: Self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) can form cores in dark matter haloes and later drive their gravothermal collapse, raising their central densities far above those of NFW haloes. Simulating this collapse is computationally demanding, requiring increasingly short time-steps and a large particle number to resolve the shrinking core. We present NORI, an $N$-body code that runs on CPUs and GPUs and simulates isolated self-gravitating systems with elastic, isotropic self-interactions using a Monte-Carlo method. To avoid MPI communication in the tree walks, each compute node in CPU mode, and each GPU or group of GPUs in GPU mode, holds a copy of all particles and its own Morton-ordered tree. A fixed evaluation order makes the results bitwise identical on CPUs and GPUs. Applying scattering events sequentially conserves the momentum and energy of each pair to round-off and allows multiple scatterings per particle per step. With global time-steps in both codes, NORI is several times faster on CPUs than our AREPO-2 implementation. One four-A100 GPU node is up to 60 times faster per time-step than AREPO-2 on a 112-core CPU node. For a full core-collapse simulation, where AREPO-2 uses individual time-steps, the speed-up is $\sim 30$ for the central density to reach $10^3$--$10^4$ times the NFW scale density, $ρ_{\rm s}$. We follow a halo with $10^8$ particles within $r_{200}$ to a central density of $10^4\,ρ_{\rm s}$. Within the limit of about $2\times10^9$ particles set by data replication, NORI provides an efficient tool for isolated SIDM simulations, including gravothermal collapse and cluster collisions.

</details>


### [90] [Focal plane modal estimation of high-order wavefront drifts on coronagraphic testbeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38014)
*Saikrishna Manojkumar,Susan F. Redmond,Leonid Pogorelyuk,Christine L. Page,Garreth Ruane,Niyati Desai,Kerri Cahoy*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出模态暗区维持（MDZM）算法，利用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）在变形镜（DM）执行器基上估计漂移波前模态，直接从探针成对图像获取DM指令，无需EFC及其正则化参数，在仿真与JPL DST硬件上实现约3×10⁻¹⁰归一化强度并维持超过36小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来的宜居世界天文台（HWO）要在长时间观测中保持精确波前稳定性，以直接成像比宿主星暗10个数量级的系外类地行星。热与机械不稳定性会引起缓慢演化的高阶波前漂移，最终削弱对比度。已有的暗区维持（DZM）策略存在不足，需要更优的焦面高阶波前传感与控制方案。

Method: 提出模态暗区维持（MDZM）：用扩展卡尔曼滤波在用于控制的DM执行器基中估计漂移的波前模态；估计器从探针成对图像中提取全焦面信息，弥补DZM的缺陷，并直接求解DM指令，从而省去EFC及其正则化参数。在仿真及NASA JPL的Decadal Survey Testbed（DST）硬件上验证；在DST上进行覆盖曝光时间与漂移率的参数扫描以评估鲁棒性；并针对HWO真实观测场景，用基于DST模型的FALCO软件包，在10%带宽宽带传感图像下做仿真，与窄带图像下的MDZM结果比较。

Result: 实验中在超过36小时内将归一化强度（NI）维持在约3×10⁻¹⁰，相比DZM缩小了与光子极限理论界之间的差距；MDZM还能给出测试台上自然漂移的估计，具备系统辨识潜力；参数扫描给出了不同曝光时间与漂移率下的鲁棒性数据；宽带（10%）与窄带（2.5%）传感图像的仿真对比结果有待正文给出。

Conclusion: MDZM是一种有效的焦面高阶波前传感与控制策略，可在长时间尺度上维持高对比度暗区，并具备系统辨识能力；其鲁棒性和面向HWO宽带观测场景的适用性正在通过DST参数扫描与FALCO宽带仿真进一步检验。

Abstract: The upcoming Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO) requires precise wavefront stability over long observation periods to directly image Exo-Earths, which are 10 orders of magnitude dimmer than their host star. Dark zone maintenance (DZM) has been proposed as a focal plane high-order wavefront sensing and control (HOWFSC) strategy to mitigate thermal and mechanical instabilities that cause slow-evolving high-order wavefront drifts, which ultimately degrade contrast. Here we present a modal dark zone maintenance (MDZM) algorithm that uses an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) to estimate the drifting wavefront modes in the basis of the deformable mirror (DM) actuators used for control. The estimator derives information across the entire focal plane from probe-pair images, addressing deficiencies of DZM, and directly obtains a DM command, eliminating the need for Electric Field Conjugation (EFC) and its regularization parameter. MDZM has been demonstrated in simulation and in hardware on the Decadal Survey Testbed (DST) at NASA JPL. These experiments have maintained a normalized intensity (NI) of $\sim 3 \times 10^{-10}$ for over 36 hours, and have reduced the gap to the photon-derived theoretical bounds compared to DZM. MDZM also provides an estimate of naturally occurring drift on the testbed, offering potential system identification capabilities. To evaluate the robustness of MDZM, a parameter scan is conducted on DST, spanning exposure times and drift rates. Additionally, realistic observing scenarios for HWO will involve broadband images with a 20% bandwidth, rather than the 2.5% narrowband images previously used in MDZM testbed demonstrations. Therefore, we tested MDZM in simulation using broadband sensing images with a 10% bandwidth, using the FALCO software package with a model based on DST, and compared its performance against MDZM runs using narrowband images.

</details>


### [91] [Experimental Demonstration of Double-Fourier Interferometer Imaging for Future Space-Based Far-Infrared Observatories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38105)
*Jeremy P. Scott,Locke D. Spencer,Chris Benson,David Naylor,Berke Ricketti,Dinula De Silva,Vince Weiler*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文在0.3–2.1 THz实验室测试平台上验证了双傅里叶干涉仪（DFI）的关键观测技术，包括定标、孔径合成、空间依赖光谱变化的探测及宽场成像，结果与理论预测高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远红外天文学对恒星形成、活动星系核和原行星系统的观测至关重要，但受衍射极限限制，空间分辨率很差。双傅里叶干涉仪（将空间干涉仪与傅里叶变换光谱仪耦合）被提出可实现高分辨率宽带光谱成像，然而此前仅有各种概念性和原型研究，缺乏对DFI能力的全面演示。

Method: 搭建工作在0.3–2.1 THz光学带宽的实验室测试平台；给出仪器的理想响应模型，并实施强度不平衡、非线性相位误差和相位中心偏移等必要定标；利用一维和二维目标开展孔径合成实验；对扩展源施加低通光学滤波器以检验对空间依赖光谱变化的敏感性；通过组合探测器阵列中不同像素（各自对应源平面不同区域）的数据实现宽场成像。

Result: 实验成功实现了一维和二维目标的孔径合成，准确重建了源几何结构；证实仪器对空间依赖的光谱变化具有敏感性；验证了宽场成像能力。实验数据与理论预测显著一致，少量偏差可归因于光学像差和透视效应。

Conclusion: 这些实验室演示验证了DFI架构是一种能力强大且成熟的技术方案，适用于未来天基远红外天文台。

Abstract: Far-infrared (FIR) astronomy is crucial for observing star formation, active galactic nuclei, and protoplanetary systems, but its effectiveness is severely limited by poor diffraction-limited spatial resolution. To overcome this limitation, a double-Fourier interferometer (DFI), which couples a spatial interferometer with a Fourier transform spectrometer, has been proposed to deliver high-resolution broadband spectral imaging. Despite various conceptual and prototype studies, a comprehensive demonstration of DFI capabilities has been lacking. In this study, we present the validation of key DFI observing techniques using a laboratory testbed operating in the 0.3 to 2.1 THz optical bandwidth. We detail the instrument's ideal response alongside necessary calibrations for intensity imbalances, non-linear phase errors, and phase-center offsets. Our experimental results successfully demonstrate aperture synthesis for both one- and two-dimensional targets, accurately recovering source geometries. Furthermore, we prove the instrument's sensitivity to spatially dependent spectral variations by utilizing a low-pass optical filter on extended sources. Finally, we validate wide-field imaging capabilities by combining data from independent pixels in a detector array, each sensitive to different regions of the source plane. The experimental data show significant agreement with theoretical predictions, with minor deviations attributed to optical aberrations and perspective effects. These laboratory demonstrations validate the DFI architecture as a highly capable and mature technology for future space-based FIR observatories.

</details>


### [92] [Model of the instrumental polarization induced by a rotating HWP with anti-reflective sub-wavelength structures for CMB polarimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38165)
*Ryota Takaku,Hiroaki Imada,Guillaume Patanchon,Yuki Sakurai*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文利用严格耦合波分析（RCWA）表征多层蓝宝石半波片（HWP）配合抗反射亚波长结构（SWS）的电磁响应，重点评估对原初B模搜索至关重要的仪器偏振，发现第四谐波仪器偏振约为10^-4量级，且提高总透过率比调整SWS不对称性更有助于降低仪器偏振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来CMB偏振实验需要精确控制系统误差以搜寻原初B模偏振。旋转半波片能有效调制偏振，但其非理想电磁响应会产生与HWP同步的仪器偏振，因此需要定量刻画HWP（尤其含SWS结构）的偏振响应，弄清SWS不对称性等几何因素对仪器偏振的影响。

Method: 采用严格耦合波分析（RCWA）计算多层蓝宝石HWP的透射率、偏振效率和仪器偏振响应。构建三种不对称SWS模型进行对比，并进一步评估一个实际制备的SWS几何（其不对称性会增强延迟量），将其响应与具有同类不对称性的理想化模型进行比较。

Result: 三种不对称SWS模型在透射率、偏振效率及仪器偏振响应上未发现可归因于SWS不对称类型的显著差异；与CMB偏振重建相关的第四谐波仪器偏振在CMB频段通常为10^-4量级，预期可被抑制。实际SWS与理想模型的比较显示，较高透射率往往对应较低的仪器偏振。

Conclusion: 改善总透过率（包括实际制备SWS轮廓的影响）可能比调整SWS不对称性本身对降低HWP引起的仪器偏振更重要，为HWP设计与系统误差控制提供指导。

Abstract: Future CMB polarization experiments require precise control of instrumental systematics in the search for primordial $B$-mode polarization. A rotating half-wave plate (HWP) provides effective polarization modulation, but its non-ideal electromagnetic response can generate HWP-synchronous instrumental polarization. In this work, we characterize the response of a multi-layer sapphire HWP with anti-reflective sub-wavelength structures (SWS) using rigorous coupled-wave analysis. For three asymmetric SWS models, we find no substantial differences in the transmittance, polarization efficiency, or instrumental-polarization response that can be attributed to the type of SWS asymmetry. The fourth-harmonic instrumental polarization relevant to CMB polarization reconstruction is typically of order $10^{-4}$ in the CMB bands, which is expected to be mitigated. We further evaluate an actually fabricated SWS geometry, whose asymmetry enhances the retardance, and compare its response with that of an idealized model having the same type of asymmetry. Comparison of the realistic SWS with the idealized model shows that higher transmittance tends to be associated with lower instrumental polarization. These results suggest that improving the total transmission, including the effect of the fabricated SWS profile, may be more important for reducing HWP-induced instrumental polarization than tuning the SWS asymmetry itself.

</details>


### [93] [Characterisation of the ILMT 4K x 4K TDI CCD imager](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38362)
*Gokul singh Mehra,Bhavya Ailawadhi,Talat Akhunov,Monalisa Dubey,Naveen Dukiya,Sara Filali,Paul Hickson,Priyanshi Kumari,Kuntal Misra,Vibhore Negi,Kumar Pranshu,Jean Surdej,Sarvesh Kumar Yadav*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文针对4米国际液体镜望远镜（ILMT）所用4K×4K CCD成像仪，利用晨曦期间非均匀照明的CCD帧，通过两种方法测定其增益（约3.9 e/ADU）与读出噪声（1.55 ADU，约6 e）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ILMT为天顶指向望远镜，CCD在TDI模式下工作，需准确标定CCD性能参数（增益与读出噪声）才能实现精确测光与图像校准，而这些参数尚待确定。

Method: 利用晨曦上升阶段获得的非均匀照明CCD帧，采用两种方法：（1）在水平梯度最小的行上直接测量通量方差，尽管存在强垂直照明梯度；（2）将CCD帧划分为背景照明相近的区域，分析两区域间通量比的变化。数学上建立信号方差与平均通量之间的线性关系以确定增益，读出噪声则由偏置帧独立测得。

Result: 两种方法均给出增益约3.9 e/ADU；由偏置帧测得的读出噪声为1.55 ADU（约6 e）。

Conclusion: 该参数估计有助于更好地理解CCD性能并实现精确校准，为ILMT的测光数据处理奠定基础。

Abstract: In this work, we determine the gain and read-out noise of the 4Kx4K CCD imager used with the 4-m International Liquid Mirror Telescope (ILMT). Due to the zenith-pointing characteristic of the telescope, the CCD operates in the Time Delay Integration (TDI) mode for image acquisition. The CCD frames obtained during rising twilight have non-uniform illumination and can potentially be used to calculate important CCD parameters using two approaches. The first approach directly measures variance in flux along rows with minimum horizontal gradient, despite the strong vertical illumination gradient. The second method divides the CCD frame into regions exposed to similar background illumination and analyses the flux ratio variation between the two regions. Mathematically, we establish a linear relationship between the signal variance and average flux, from which gain is determined, while the read-out noise (RON) is measured independently from the bias frames. Both methods provide a gain value of approximately 3.9 e/ADU, while the RON measured from bias frames is 1.55 ADU (~ 6 e). Estimation of these parameters provides a better understanding of the CCD performance and allows for an accurate calibration.

</details>


### [94] [PyMsOfa 2.0: A Pure-Python Implementation of the IAU Standards of Fundamental Astronomy Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38410)
*Dongjie Tan,Jianghui Ji,Jiacheng Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: PyMsOfa 2.0 将 IAU SOFA 全部 247 个官方例程用纯 Python + NumPy 重写，无需编译 C 库，跨平台一致运行，支持数组批量运算，并附扩展模块，经验证与官方 C 版本在机器精度内一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文测量（astrometry）计算需要权威、可复现的算法，而 IAU 的 SOFA 官方仅以 Fortran 77 和 ANSI C 发布，Python 用户使用不便；此前 PyMsOfa 1.0 只是对 SOFA C 库的封装，仍需编译外部 C 库，跨平台部署困难。

Method: 作者把 SOFA 源文件中的全部常量、级数和系数原样迁移，将 247 个官方例程逐一直接重写为纯 Python 实现（例程名加 pym 前缀），底层基于 NumPy 数组，从而支持参数批量处理；另设扩展模块，纳入尚未进入官方 SOFA 发布的已验证模型（如 IAU 2006_J2/2000A_R26 改进岁差—章动模型）。

Result: 逐例程与官方 SOFA C 版本对比验证：多数例程达到机器精度一致，其余例程的偏差均在 SOFA 文档所述精度容差内；包可在 Linux、Windows、macOS 上完全相同地运行，无需编译任何外部 C 库；已应用于 Gaia 天体测量归算与 CHES（Closeby Habitable Exoplanet Survey）任务的天体测量研究，并通过 PyPI、GitHub、Zenodo 发布。

Conclusion: 纯 Python/NumPy 的 SOFA 重实现在大批量巡天管线与蒙特卡洛模拟中兼具正确性、可复现性与易部署性，可作为官方 C/Fortran 版本之外可靠的 Python 替代方案，并可通过扩展模块接入前沿模型。

Abstract: Astrometric calculations require authoritative algorithms so that every result is correct and reproducible. The Standards of Fundamental Astronomy (SOFA) service of the International Astronomical Union (IAU) provides such algorithms, officially released only in Fortran 77 and ANSI C. In an earlier paper we presented the first version of PyMsOfa, a Python package wrapping the SOFA C library. Here we present PyMsOfa 2.0, which re-implements the complete SOFA library in pure Python with NumPy. All 247 official SOFA routines are ported directly to Python, requiring no compilation of any external C library and running identically on Linux, Windows, and macOS. The routines operate on NumPy arrays, so arguments can be processed in batch, which is convenient for survey pipelines and Monte Carlo simulations. The package also provides an extension module with validated models not yet part of the official SOFA release, such as the improved precession--nutation model IAU 2006$_{J_2}$/2000A$_{R26}$. The reimplementation reproduces the official SOFA algorithms: all constants, series, and coefficients are taken unchanged from the SOFA source files, and routine names differ only by the pym prefix. We verify every routine against the official SOFA C release: most reproduce the C results to machine precision, the remainder within the accuracy tolerance stated in the SOFA documentation. The package has been applied to Gaia astrometric reduction and used in astrometric studies of the Closeby Habitable Exoplanet Survey (CHES) mission, and is available through PyPI, GitHub, and Zenodo.

</details>


### [95] [Shock detection in meshless finite mass hydrodynamic schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38700)
*Ludwig M. Böss,Frederik Groth,Klaus Dolag,Damiano Caprioli*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该工作首次为无网格有限质量（MFM）流体方案实现了带激波面重构的激波识别算法，并在宇宙结构形成模拟中提升了激波能量耗散与宇宙线加速的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激波广泛存在于天体物理等离子体中，在结构形成过程中把引力势能转化为热、湍流、磁场与粒子加速；但模拟中激波相互重叠碰撞、嵌于复杂剪切流中，准确捕捉十分困难，且全拉格朗日流体方法历来逊于欧拉/伪拉格朗日方法，作者希望弥补这一差距。

Method: 首次针对MFM（meshless finite mass）流体格式实现激波识别（shockfinder）算法，并进行激波面重构；将其与解析预测以及基于SPH的旧版激波识别方法进行对比验证，随后应用于宇宙结构形成模拟。

Result: 该MFM激波识别方法在精度上与解析预测相符，并相对基于SPH的传统激波识别方法表现更优；应用于宇宙结构形成模拟后，改进了对激波能量耗散和宇宙线加速的预测。

Conclusion: 结果表明MFM方案配合激波面重构可有效提升拉格朗日类方法在复杂激波环境下的识别能力，为宇宙结构形成模拟中的激波耗散与宇宙线加速研究提供更可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Shocks are prevalent in a multitude of astrophysical plasmas. They are essential in the dissipation of gravitational potential energy during structure formation, where they channel it into heat, turbulence, magnetic fields, and particle acceleration. Accurately capturing shocks in simulations of cosmological structure formation is a considerable challenge, due to the complexity of overlapping and colliding shocks, embedded in a medium experiencing complex shear flows. Fully Lagrangian hydrodynamical methods have historically been inferior in this regard compared to Eulerian or pseudo-Lagrangian methods. This work aims to remedy this discrepancy. We present the first efforts to implement a shockfinder algorithm with shock surface reconstruction for meshless finite mass (MFM) hydrodynamic schemes. We show its accuracy compared to analytic predictions and a legacy smoothed particle hydrodynamics (SPH)- based shock-finding method. We apply this new shock-finding method to simulations of cosmic structure formation to obtain improved predictions for shock energy dissipation and cosmic ray (CR) acceleration.

</details>


### [96] [lenscape: Fast, High-resolution $Δχ^2$ Maps of Microlensing Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38764)
*Michael Hippke*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: lenscape 是一个开源 Python 包，通过多阶段邻域传播与自适应等值线细化，加速微引力透镜 Δχ² 图的构建并抑制局部极小伪影；在 MOA-2022-BLG-091 上复现 50×50 图仅需 14 分钟，400×400 图不到两小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星微引力透镜分析依赖在双透镜分离 s 与质量比 q 平面上的 Δχ² 图来定位简并解并约束参数不确定性，但每个网格点都需进行包含有限源放大率的完整光变曲线优化，构建此类图成为事件建模的主要计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出开源 Python 软件包 lenscape，采用多阶段邻域传播策略（利用相邻网格点的解作为初值）结合自适应等值线细化，以加速 Δχ² 映射并抑制落入局部极小的伪影。

Result: 应用于行星事件 MOA-2022-BLG-091：在普通桌面计算机上 14 分钟内复现已发表的 50×50 Δχ² 图，并可在两小时内生成 400×400 的高分辨率图。

Conclusion: lenscape 显著降低了微引力透镜 Δχ² 制图的计算成本，使高分辨率简并结构探索在常规硬件上变得可行，具备作为事件建模通用工具的潜力。

Abstract: Planetary microlensing analyses rely on $Δχ^2$ maps across the binary-lens separation $s$ and mass ratio $q$ to locate degenerate solutions and bound parameter uncertainties. Because every grid point requires a full light-curve optimization with finite-source magnifications, constructing these maps is often the primary computational bottleneck in event modeling. Here I present lenscape, an open-source Python package that accelerates $Δχ^2$ mapping and suppresses local-minimum artifacts through multi-stage neighbor propagation and adaptive contour refinement. Applied to the planetary event MOA-2022-BLG-091, lenscape reproduces the published $50\times50$ map in 14 minutes and generates a $400\times400$ map in under two hours on a desktop computer.

</details>


### [97] [UnEZ: A Fully Unsupervised Self-Templating Neural Network for Redshift and Line Strength Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38816)
*Asha Abd,Karl Glazebrook,Colin Jacobs*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 提出全无监督、物理先验驱动的机器学习模型UnEZ，无需任何红移或线强标签即可预测光谱红移与谱线强度，性能可与现代红移估计方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模光谱巡天快速发展，使金属丰度、恒星形成率、红移等星系属性的估计计算代价高；现有方法依赖模板光谱或人工整理的红移星表，难以代表大巡天中天体的多样性。

Method: 全无监督、物理先验的神经网络：编码器预测发射线与吸收线的振幅/宽度，固定的确定性解码器据此构造唯一模板，并给出估计红移、红移置信曲线与光谱模型；在SDSS DR18上以独立红移星表为基准测试。

Result: 在SNR>10时外点率为0.459%、NMAD为1.237×10⁻⁴；SNR>3时外点率3.89%、NMAD为1.471×10⁻⁴。此外，其线强与红移预测可用于构建传统算法所需的模板光谱。

Conclusion: UnEZ表明全无监督神经网络可让红移等星系属性从光谱本身自然涌现，提供免模板、免标签且可扩展至未来巡天需求的方法。

Abstract: As spectroscopic surveys grow, estimating galaxy properties such as metallicity, star formation rate, and redshift becomes computationally cumbersome. Methods of estimating such properties rely on template spectra or curated redshift catalogs, which fail to represent the diversity of objects seen in large-scale surveys. We present UnEZ, a fully unsupervised, physics-informed machine learning model that can predict spectroscopic redshifts and line strengths with no prior redshift or line strength labels. An encoder predicts emission and absorption line amplitudes/widths, and a fixed deterministic decoder constructs a unique template, estimated redshift, redshift confidence curve, and spectral model. UnEZ was tested on the Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS) Data Release 18 due to its large dataset and thoroughly vetted redshift catalog which serves as an independent benchmark. Despite the lack of templates, line strength labels, or redshift labels, UnEZ's performance competes with modern redshift estimation algorithms. At SNR > 10, UnEZ achieves an outlier fraction of 0.459% and a normalized median absolute deviation (NMAD) of $1.237 \times 10^{-4}$. At SNR > 3, UnEZ achieves an outlier fraction of 3.89% and an NMAD of $1.471 \times 10^{-4}$. Furthermore, we demonstrate how UnEZ's line strength and redshift predictions can be utilized for various purposes such as constructing template spectra required in traditional redshift estimation algorithms. UnEZ illustrates how a fully unsupervised neural network can allow redshift, among other galaxy properties, to emerge naturally from the spectra themselves, providing a template-free, label-free approach that scales to the demands of future surveys.

</details>


### [98] [SuperCrude EoS: A Simple, Analytical, and Reasonably Accurate Equation of State for Astrophysical Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38848)
*Paul C. Duffell,Dillon Hasenour*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperCrude：一种大幅简化的天体物理物态方程，用紧凑的、物理动机明确的解析近似替代精确插值数据表，捕获爆炸天体物理所需的最重要物理，并保证热力学严格一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精确物态方程（如helmeos）依赖插值数据表，复杂且不易保证热力学一致性；而爆炸天体物理模拟只需要抓住最关键的物理。作者希望用解析近似构建一个简洁、一致、足够准确的替代方案。

Method: 用解析的、物理动机明确的近似公式表达物态方程，取代精确插值表；通过设计使求解器天然满足热力学一致性；并用一系列代码测试在广泛参数空间内评估其表现，再将SuperCrude耦合进流体力学与天体物理测试问题（包括白矮星热核爆炸）中。

Result: 在很大一部分参数空间中，SuperCrude的精度达到helmeos的水平；在简并、气体与辐射可比拟的部分区域，其有效绝热指数的误差约为百分之几；温度估计精度较低，误差约为数十个百分点。与刚性核网络耦合时，其核合成产额与helmeos不同。

Conclusion: SuperCrude以解析形式实现了高精度与严格热力学一致性，适用于大范围爆炸天体物理问题；但温度精度不足导致核合成产额与helmeos存在差异，未来需进一步改进温度精度。

Abstract: We present SuperCrude, a vastly simplified astrophysical equation of state that captures the most important physics needed for explosive astrophysics, but using compact, physically-motivated analytical approximations rather than precise interpolated data tables. We present a number of code tests demonstrating the performance of SuperCrude in a wide range of parameter space. In large regions of parameter space, SuperCrude is precisely accurate to within the accuracy level of helmeos. For some regions (especially where degeneracy, gas, and radiation are comparable) SuperCrude has errors in its effective adiabatic index on the order of a few percent. The solver's estimate of the temperature is less accurate, on the order of tens of percent. Because the solver is analytic, SuperCrude is designed in such a way to guarantee exact thermodynamic consistency. We incorporate SuperCrude into some hydrodynamic and astrophysical test problems, including the thermonuclear explosion of a white dwarf star. When coupled with a stiff nuclear network, SuperCrude does not achieve the same nucleosynthetic yields as helmeos, motivating future work to improve the temperature accuracy further.

</details>


### [99] [Flow-based neural networks for scalable high resolution imaging of VLBI observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38978)
*Lev Kats,Alexey Goldin,Andrey S. Andrianov*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该文将WaveletFlow神经网络架构引入甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）的贝叶斯成像，以缓解以往流模型随恢复特征数增长带来的计算开销，并在模型数据、RadioAstron对M87的存档观测和ALMA对HL Tau的观测上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLBI贝叶斯采样成像可实现极高角分辨率的可靠成像，但从经典MCMC过渡到基于流的神经网络后，随着恢复特征（像素）数量增加，计算成本依然高昂，存在可扩展性问题。

Method: 采用WaveletFlow神经网络架构，利用其小波域多尺度生成结构提升可恢复图像的像素规模，从而扩展此前模型的尺度适应能力；随后将算法应用于合成模型数据、RadioAstron项目对M87的存档观测数据以及ALMA对HL Tau的观测数据。

Result: 在单幅图像的保真度上与CLEAN相当，但能提供更丰富的统计信息；同时相比此前的流模型可支持更高的像素数量，缓解了扩展性问题。

Conclusion: WaveletFlow可提升VLBI贝叶斯成像的可扩展性，其提供的统计信息对活动星系核等致密天体的未来研究可能具有重要意义。

Abstract: Bayesian sampling methods in very-long-baseline interferometry enable reliable imaging of astronomical sources with a record high angular resolution. However, even after the transition from classical MCMC methods to flow-based neural networks, they remain computationally expensive as the number of recovered features grows. We used WaveletFlow neural network architecture to increase the number of pixels in the recovered image distribution, addressing the scaling issues of previous models. We applied the algorithm to both model data and archive RadioAstron project observations of M87 and ALMA telescope observations of HL Tau. While yielding comparable fidelity of the individual images, the algorithm provides more statistical information compared to CLEAN, which may be crucial for future research on compact astronomical objects such as active galactic nuclei.

</details>


### [100] [The Korean Space Telescope (KST): A Dedicated Space Observatory for the Dark Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39094)
*M. James Jee,Ho Seong Hwang,Dongwook Lim,Minjin Kim,Myungshin Im,Sungwook E. Hong,Ho-Gyu Lee,Soung-Chul Yang,David Parkinson,Arman Shafieloo,Jun-Qian Jiang,Haeng Pal Heo,Dongok Ryu,Hagyong Kihm,Hyung Yun Noh,Wooyong Kang,Youngsun Kim*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍韩国空间望远镜（KST）：一台1.4米宽视场专用暗物质与暗能量探测空间天文台，通过可见光衍射极限成像与同步近红外无缝光谱实现"自足式"巡天，联合引力透镜、重子声波振荡、Ia型超新星和引力波暗警报钟，系统解答暗能量、暗物质本质与哈勃常数三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物理的三大悬而未决问题——暗能量动力学、暗物质本质、以及作为独立宇宙学标尺的引力波"暗警报钟"——需要同时具备大面积、高分辨率成像与近红外光谱能力的专用设施；现有及在建的Rubin、Euclid、Roman各有侧重，仍存在能力缺口，尤其缺乏对每个探测源同步提供近红外光谱信息以支撑时域科学的手段。

Method: KST搭载单一仪器，同时提供衍射极限可见光成像（光学波段约0.15角秒）与近红外无缝光谱（R~100），使每个探测源在巡天中即可获得光谱信息，无需单独后续观测；巡天采用双层"婚礼蛋糕"策略，即2000平方度的宽场巡天加10平方度的星团定向深度巡天，结合弱引力透镜、重子声波振荡和近红外标准化的Ia型超新星哈勃图，并从星系团核心到星系子结构绘制暗物质分布。

Result: 在暗能量转折红移处可将w的约束精度达到σ(w_p)~0.03；通过暗物质分布测量区分冷暗物质与温暗物质、自相互作用暗物质等替代模型；构建深近红外宿主星系目录，给出不依赖距离梯的H_0测量；同时以零额外观测成本产出星系演化、活动星系核、系外行星和银河系等遗产科学成果。

Conclusion: KST作为韩国首个专用天体物理空间任务，以"成像+同步近红外光谱"的一体化设计和双层巡天策略，同时攻坚暗能量的动力学、暗物质的本质与引力波暗警报钟三种暗物理前沿问题，并与Rubin、Euclid、Roman形成互补而非重叠的独特定位。

Abstract: The Korean Space Telescope (KST) is a next-generation 1.4-meter wide-field space observatory, a dedicated probe of the dark sector. Its single instrument provides diffraction-limited visible imaging (~0.15 arcsec in the optical) together with simultaneous near-infrared (R ~ 100) slitless spectrophotometry, so that every detected source is observed in the NIR by the survey itself, with spectral information delivered at a resolution set by its brightness (full R ~ 100 typing at the bright end, synthetic medium-band or broadband photometry below it) and with no separate follow-up, a capability decisive for time-domain science. With it, KST addresses the three outstanding questions of dark physics in concert: the dynamics of dark energy, the nature of dark matter, and gravitational-wave dark sirens as independent cosmological rulers. KST combines measurements of expansion and structure growth to test dark-energy models. A two-tiered "Wedding Cake" strategy (2,000 deg^2 Wide plus 10 deg^2 cluster-targeted Deep Survey) combines weak lensing, baryon acoustic oscillations, and a near-infrared-standardized Type Ia supernova Hubble diagram to constrain dark energy at its pivot redshift (sigma(w_p) ~ 0.03); maps dark matter from cluster cores to galactic substructure to test cold dark matter against warm and self-interacting alternatives; and builds a deep near-infrared host catalog for a distance-ladder-independent H_0 from dark sirens. The same data yield a Legacy Science portfolio (galaxy evolution, active galactic nuclei, exoplanets, and the Milky Way) at no additional observing cost. As Korea's first dedicated astrophysics space mission, KST occupies a niche complementary to the Vera C. Rubin Observatory, Euclid, and Roman.

</details>


### [101] [Tabular Foundation Models Dramatically Outperform Bespoke Photometric Redshift Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39332)
*Biprateep Dey,Joshua S. Speagle*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该文提出用表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文学习从成像数据估计光度红移，仅需1000个训练样本即可媲美或超越用4万余星系训练的专用估计器，且在标定数据稀缺时优势最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光度红移对河外天体物理与宇宙学研究至关重要，但数据驱动估计器依赖光谱训练集，而这类数据稀缺且系统性地偏向明亮、易观测的星系，导致选择偏差问题。

Method: 采用表格基础模型，其合成预训练相当于学习到关于回归问题的先验；在广泛使用的基准数据集上以纯上下文学习方式，单次前向传播输出完整概率分布，并在代表性训练数据与有偏训练数据两种情形下与专用估计器对比。

Result: TFM在两类数据情形下均系统性优于专用估计器；仅用1000个上下文样本即可匹配或超过用超过40000个星系训练的基线；当几乎没有标定数据时优势最大，且即使基线额外获得多达10000条代表性标定光谱，该优势依然存在。

Conclusion: TFM为未来纯成像巡天提供了样本高效的框架，只需在上下文中加入少量代表性标定样本，即可缓解观测选择偏差。

Abstract: Estimating galaxy redshifts (a proxy for distance) from imaging data alone, also known as photometric redshifts (photo-$z$'s), is essential for studies of extragalactic astrophysics and cosmology. However, data-driven estimators rely on spectroscopic training datasets, which are scarce and systematically biased toward bright and easily observed galaxies. In this work, we show that tabular foundation models (TFMs), whose synthetic pretraining amounts to a learned prior over regression problems, can outperform bespoke estimators using only in-context learning. Evaluating on a widely used benchmark dataset, we find that TFMs predicting full probability distributions in a single forward pass systematically outperform bespoke estimators across both representative and biased training data regimes, matching or exceeding baselines trained on over $40{,}000$ galaxies with just $1{,}000$ training context examples. We also show that the TFM advantage is largest when little to no calibration data are available, and that it persists even when the bespoke baselines are supplemented with up to $10{,}000$ representative calibration spectra. This shows that TFMs offer a sample-efficient framework for upcoming imaging-only astronomical surveys, where observational selection bias can be mitigated simply by including a small representative calibration sample in context.

</details>


### [102] [Towards an Astronomical Use of New-Generation Geodetic Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39656)
*Víctor Pérez-Díez*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一套将VGOS相关器输出转化为全极化、校准后可见度的处理流程，用于多频射电成像与偏振分析，并将其用于blazar 1803+784等6个AGN，测量核心位移、谱指数和法拉第旋转，最后用重建的源模型改正测地VLBI中的结构效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGOS是为提高地面/天球参考架精度和地球定向参数而设计的下一代测地VLBI网络，但其宽频覆盖和对致密AGN的规律监测为天体物理研究提供了机会，此前缺乏将其数据用于天文成像与偏振分析的完整校准流程。

Method: 构建校准管线：线偏振到圆偏振转换、仪器相位与幅度校准、考虑电离层色散效应的新型宽带整体条纹拟合算法，再用正则化最大似然成像重建多频强度与偏振图像；应用于1803+784及3C418、0059+581、0613+570、1849+670、2229+695，拟合频率相关的核心位移、谱指数与法拉第旋转；并将源模型用于改正测地VLBI观测量的结构依赖效应。

Result: 测得频率相关的核心位移与谱指数；1803+784的核心位移指数为 k_r = 0.73^{+0.12}_{-0.19}，偏离简单锥形喷流且处于能量均分假设的情况。将重建源模型用于结构改正后，对强结构源（尤其3C418）可降低闭合延迟散布与拟合后残差，效果依赖源的可变性及成像观测的时间接近程度。

Conclusion: 结果体现了VGOS的双重科学价值：既是强大的多频天文VLBI仪器，也能改善测地测量精度。

Abstract: This thesis explores the astronomical use of observations from the VLBI Global Observing System (VGOS), a next-generation geodetic network designed to improve the precision of terrestrial and celestial reference frames and Earth orientation parameters. Although primarily conceived for geodesy, its wide frequency coverage and regular monitoring of compact active galactic nuclei (AGN) provide valuable opportunities for astrophysics.
  We develop a calibration pipeline that transforms VGOS correlator outputs into full-polarization, calibrated visibilities suitable for astronomical imaging. The procedure combines linear-to-circular polarization conversion, instrumental phase and amplitude calibration, and a new wideband global fringe-fitting algorithm that accounts for dispersive ionospheric effects. Regularized maximum likelihood imaging is then used to reconstruct multi-frequency intensity and polarimetric images.
  The pipeline is applied to the blazar 1803+784 and five additional AGN: 3C418, 0059+581, 0613+570, 1849+670, and 2229+695. We measure frequency-dependent core shifts, spectral indices, and Faraday rotation properties. For 1803+784, the fitted core-shift index is $k_r = 0.73^{+0.12}_{-0.19}$, suggesting departures from a simple conical jet in energy equipartition.
  Finally, we apply the reconstructed source models to correct structure-dependent effects in geodetic VLBI observables. The correction reduces closure-delay scatter and post-fit residuals for strongly structured sources, particularly 3C418, while its effectiveness depends on source variability and the temporal proximity of the imaging observations.
  These results demonstrate the dual scientific value of VGOS as a powerful multi-frequency astronomical VLBI instrument and as a means of improving geodetic measurements.

</details>


### [103] [Search for Cosmic Rays in GRANDProto300](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39720)
*Jolan Lavoisier,Aurélien Benoit-Lévy,Kumiko Kotera,Mauricio Bustamante,Marion Guelfand,Nathan Lebas,Takashi Sako,Xishui Tian,for the GRAND Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: GRANDProto300 用 46–63 个自主无线电天线探测 10^17–10^18.5 eV 宇宙线，介绍了包含四步滤波的搜索流程并评估其效率，同时展示正在开发的基于信号波形的 CNN 噪声/宇宙线甄别模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证 GRAND 实验的自主无线电探测技术，并借此研究银河系内到河外源的过渡能区（10^17–10^18.5 eV）。

Method: 2024 年 11 月至 2025 年 6 月运行 46 个天线，2025 年 7 月起 63 个天线稳定取数；宇宙线搜索流程包含四个滤波步骤：(1) 剔除时间与空间成簇的噪声事件，(2) 偏振切割，(3) 天顶角到达方向切割，(4) 相对预期触发时刻的延迟切割。此外开发了一层基于波形迹线的 CNN 算法用于区分噪声与宇宙线信号，模型分别在模拟数据与真实宇宙线信号数据集上训练。

Result: 滤波流程的效率在 2025 年 7 月至 10 月的最新一批数据上进行了评估；CNN 模型在模拟和真实宇宙线数据集上训练并给出了准确率。

Conclusion: GRANDProto300 已具备规模化稳定运行与宇宙线搜索能力，滤波加机器学习的组合有望提升自主无线电探测的噪声抑制与信号识别性能。

Abstract: GRANDProto300 is a prototype array of the GRAND experiment, designed to validate the technique of autonomous radio-detection of astroparticles by detecting cosmic rays with energies between 10$^{17} - 10^{18.5}$ eV. This observation will further enable the study of the Galactic-to-extragalactic source transition region. Between November 2024 and June 2025, 46 antennas were operational, and since July 2025, 63 antennas have been collecting data stably. We present here our cosmic-ray search pipeline, that involves several filtering steps: (1) exclusion of clustered (in time and space) noise events, (2) polarization cut, (3) zenith arrival cut, and (4) delay in expected trigger cut. The efficiency is evaluated on the latest batch of data from July 2025 to October 2025. Additionally, we present an identification layer that is being developed: a CNN-based algorithm to discriminate between noise and cosmic-ray signals, using signal traces. We present the accuracy of our model, trained both on simulations and on real cosmic-ray signal datasets.

</details>


### [104] [The Radio Neutrino Observatory in Greenland (RNO-G) - System Design and Instrumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39740)
*Nils Heyer for the RNO-G Collaboration,Steven Barwick,AJ Nielsen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: RNO-G通过天线阵列探测EeV能段宇宙中微子产生的纳秒级射电脉冲；本文概述其电子学信号链，并介绍用于将LPDA天线熔装入格陵兰冰盖的新型电热熔器，可将定位不确定度降至±2 cm（水平）和±1°（旋转），并报告了Summit站现场的测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EeV能段宇宙中微子通量极低，需要超大体积探测介质；格陵兰冰盖提供了合适的大体积靶，而射电探测可覆盖纳秒级短脉冲。同时，天线在冰内的精确安装与定位对阵列性能至关重要，传统安装方式定位不确定性较大，因此需要改进布放设备。

Method: 介绍RNO-G电子学系统从天线到记录脉冲的完整测量流程；研发电热熔器（electric melters）用于将LPDA天线熔入冰盖，简化布放流程并控制天线姿态。

Result: 定位不确定度降低至±2 cm（x/y方向）和±1°（旋转角）；在Summit站的RNO-G站点开展了现场测试。

Conclusion: 电热熔器方案可简化天线布放并显著提高定位精度，为RNO-G阵列的规模化建设与射电中微子探测性能提供保障。

Abstract: The Radio Neutrino Observatory in Greenland (RNO-G) is designed to measure cosmic neutrinos in the EeV range through an array of radio antennas detecting nanosecond-long radio flashes. In this contribution, the RNO-G electronic system is outlined, illustrating how signals are measured from the antennas to the recorded pulses. We present new instrumentation developments, focusing on electric melters to install LPDA antennas into the Greenlandic ice sheet. The new system will simplify deployment and reduce positioning uncertainty to $\pm$2cm (x/y) and $\pm$1°(rotational). We report on field tests from the RNO-G site at Summit Station.

</details>


### [105] [Robust statistical significance for Gravitational Wave inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39785)
*Alexandre Sébastien Göttel*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出用"证据网络"(evidence networks)学习数据集之间的可区分性来推断贝叶斯因子，每秒可计算高达10^5个贝叶斯因子，从而构建贝叶斯因子的噪声背景分布以估计显著性，并在无偏的情况下把结果扩展到上万次事件，为不依赖高斯平稳噪声假设的无似然方法奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波数据分析（事件层面判断进动、偏心率等，以及族群层面）严重依赖贝叶斯因子，但存在三大问题：(1) 族群层面依赖事件级嵌套采样输出，随探测事件数增加，不确定性和偏差不断累积；(2) 由于贝叶斯因子在噪声下的分布未知，无法据此做可量化的统计陈述；(3) 在至少20%含有瞬态噪声伪影的事件中，贝叶斯因子的统计意义存疑。

Method: 采用证据网络：先学习不同数据集之间的可区分性，再据此推断贝叶斯因子；利用其极高计算速度（每秒最多10^5个贝叶斯因子），在不同信号假设下构建贝叶斯因子的背景分布用于显著性估计。

Result: 计算速度可达每秒10^5个贝叶斯因子；高精度使得在无可见偏差的情况下，可将结果从最多10^4次事件外推至族群层面的量；并构建了不依赖高斯、平稳噪声假设的无似然方法的初步基础。

Conclusion: 证据网络能够同时缓解贝叶斯因子分布未知、嵌套采样误差累积以及瞬态噪声下统计意义不明等问题，为引力波数据分析提供快速、可量化且无似然的新途径。

Abstract: The field of gravitational-wave data analysis heavily relies on Bayes factors to make statements about astrophysical effects in events detected by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration. This is true on an event level, for example when discussing the potential detection of precession or eccentricity, as well as on a population level. For the latter, perhaps the main limiting factor is the reliance on the output from event-level nested sampling analyses, which is known to introduce growing uncertainties and biases as the number of detections increases. In both cases, while Bayes factors are useful when comparing different models, because the underlying distribution of Bayes factors in noise is unknown, it is often not possible to use them to make quantifiable statistical statements. Their statistical meaning is also questionable in the at least $20\%$ of gravitational-wave events that contain transient noise artifacts. This paper shows that it is possible to use evidence networks, which can infer Bayes factors after learning the distinguishability between datasets, to solve all of the aforementioned problems. By being able to calculate up to $10^5$ Bayes factors per second, we can construct background distributions of Bayes factors under different signal assumptions to estimate significance. We show that the high precision achieved allows us to scale the results from up to $10^4$ events with no discernable bias on population-level quantities, while laying the foundations for a likelihood-free method that does not rely on the assumption of Gaussian and stationary noise.

</details>


### [106] [Preparation of a celestial field for precise VLBI observations of the JUICE spacecraft: a test case of the Venus flyby](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39925)
*Krisztina Perger,Sándor Frey,Giuseppe Cimò,Dominic Dirkx,Jasper Edwards,Sam Fayolle,Judit Fogasy,Krisztina É. Gabányi,Leonid I. Gurvits,Máté Krezinger,Guifré Molera Calvés,Vidhya Pallichadath,Noor Masdiana Md Said,Bert Vermeersen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 为支持JUICE任务2025年金星引力借力期间的航天器状态矢量重建，利用EVN在8.4 GHz对木星轨迹附近9个束内校准候选源和3个RFC源开展nodding式相位参考VLBI观测，将位置与ICRF源相对天体测量关联；探测到4个ICC源与全部RFC源（亚mas精度），但ICC源均因过弱或展宽而不适合作相位参考校准源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JUICE任务（2023年发射、2031年到达木星系统）的行星无线电干涉与多普勒实验在行星际巡航阶段即对航天器进行射电天文观测。由于可作为VLBI相位参考校准源的致密射电源天区密度低，沿黄道加密天球参考架是支撑JUICE的关键任务；为支持2025年8月金星借力机动时航天器状态矢量的精确重建，需要沿JUICE轨迹选取束内校准源候选。

Method: 沿JUICE金星借力轨迹天区选取9个束内校准源（ICC）候选，并尝试将射电基本星表中的3个已知较亮致密参考源与ICRF联系。使用欧洲VLBI网（EVN）在8.4 GHz开展nodding式相位参考VLBI观测，通过相对天体测量将其位置链接到邻近ICRF源。

Result: 4个ICC目标与全部3个RFC源被EVN探测并成图，坐标精度通常达亚mas量级。所观测的ICC源均不适合作VLBI相位参考校准源，原因是信号过弱或存在分辨结构；但其新测位置与流量密度原则上可用于金星借力时航天器探测的验证。详细分析发现J0843+1826在mas尺度上结构被分辨，J0843+1814是一颗年轻射电星系，可能为星系团成员。

Conclusion: 沿黄道加密参考架的需求仍未由所观测ICC源满足，需继续搜寻更亮、更致密的相位参考校准源；本次确定的源位置与流量密度可作为航天器探测的辅助验证信息，并增补了轨迹天区的射电源普查（含一个年轻射电星系候选）。

Abstract: The Jupiter Icy Moons Explorer mission, launched in 2023, is on its way to the Jovian system, to arrive in 2031. The Planetary Radio Interferometry and Doppler Experiment performs radio astronomical observations of the spacecraft already in its interplanetary cruise phase. Since the sky density of potential compact radio sources suitable as phase-reference calibrators in very long baseline interferometry observations is low, densifying the celestial reference frame along the Ecliptic is an essential task in support of JUICE. In preparation for the JUICE gravity assist manoeuvre at Venus in August 2025, to support the accurate reconstruction of the spacecraft state vector, we selected in-beam calibrator source candidates along the JUICE trajectory. Additionally, we attempted to link known nearby bright compact reference sources from the Radio Fundamental Catalog to the International Celestial Reference Frame. We performed nodding-style phase-referencing VLBI observations with the European VLBI Network at 8.4GHz, targeting nine in-beam calibrator candidate and three RFC sources in the celestial vicinity of JUICE's trajectory during its Venus flyby. We linked their positions to nearby ICRF sources by means of relative astrometry. Four of the ICC targets and all RFC sources were detected and imaged with the EVN, and their coordinates determined with typically sub-mas precision. Although none of the targeted ICC sources proved suitable as VLBI phase-reference calibrators because of being too faint or resolved, their newly-determined positions and flux densities could in principle be used to verify the detection of the spacecraft at the time of the actual flyby around Venus. During the detailed analysis of the ICC targets, we found that the structure of one of them, J0843+1826 is resolved on mas scale, and the other one, J0843+1814 is a young radio galaxy, likely a member of a cluster.

</details>


### [107] [Identifying narrow-band mutual coupling resonances in SKA-low stations using frequency-independent current basis functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40008)
*John Cumner,Oscar S. D. O'Hara,Quentin Gueuning,Anthony Brown,Fred Dulwich,Oskar Zetterstrom*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出基于频率无关电流基函数与奇异值分解的方法，用于识别孔径阵列中由互耦引起的窄带谱特征，并在SKA低频阵元与伪随机台站上验证，指出共振频段会污染延迟功率谱中的EoR窗口，同时给出了两个可用洁净频段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧密排列的孔径阵列阵元间互耦会在嵌入式阵元方向图（EEP）中引入窄带谱特征，威胁SKA-low等仪器对21厘米功率谱的测量，因此需要一种可靠手段识别这些特征并评估其对EoR观测窗口的污染。

Method: 通过对频率相关基函数作奇异值分解，构建频率无关的电流基函数，用以识别互耦导致的窄带谱特征；在由三个阵元组成的线性阵列以及SKA对数周期天线的完整伪随机台站上进行验证，并分析互耦所诱导的电流功率，进而仿真SKA-low核心区的延迟功率谱。

Result: 伪随机布局台站中观察到大量窄带共振特征，其中50.8 MHz和83.6 MHz处共振强烈，无源阵元上的耦合功率可接近甚至超过被激励阵元；仿真延迟功率谱显示，包含共振特征的观测频段会造成严重前景泄漏，遮蔽EoR窗口；采用SKALA4.0的伪随机台站在59.2–76.2 MHz与84.2–103.3 MHz两个频段不受电流共振污染，可保持EoR窗口。

Conclusion: 基于频率无关电流基函数的分解方法能有效定位孔径阵列中的互耦共振频带；避开这些频段（如59.2–76.2 MHz和84.2–103.3 MHz）有助于在SKA-low观测中保留EoR窗口，为21厘米功率谱测量提供频段选择依据。

Abstract: Mutual coupling between closely spaced elements in aperture arrays introduces narrow-band spectral features in the Embedded Element Patterns (EEPs), threatening measurements of the 21-cm power spectrum with instruments such as SKA-low. We detail a method to identify such features through the construction of frequency-independent current basis functions, utilising a singular value decomposition of frequency-dependent basis functions. We demonstrate the method on a three-element linear array and full pseudo-random station of SKA log-periodic antennas and examine the current power induced by mutual coupling. For the pseudo-random layout station we note many narrow-band resonance features, including strong resonances at 50.8~MHz and 83.6~MHz, where the coupled power on the passive antennas can approach or exceed that of the excited antenna. Simulated delay power spectra for the SKA-low core show that observing in frequency bands including resonance features causes large foreground leakage within the delay power spectra, obscuring the EoR window. For the pseudo-random layout station with SKALA4.0 we identify two significant frequency bands from 59.2--76.2~MHz and 84.2--103.3~MHz which are free of contamination from current resonances, preserving the EoR window in these frequency ranges.

</details>


### [108] [Temporal regularization of petal modes in SCAO systems: The Soft-Limiter approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40184)
*Guido Agapito,Jueqi Lin,Carmelo Arcidiacono,Giulia Carlà,Enrico Pinna,Fabio Rossi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 提出 MMSE Soft-Limiter 控制器，将基于 von Kármán 先验的子空间 MMSE 估计与连续时间正则化结合，仅对相对花瓣（petal）自由度施加泄漏积分，从而在强湍流下抑制 ELT 分段镜的差分活塞"岛屿效应"，同时不牺牲连续模式校正性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大型望远镜采用分段主镜和厚支撑结构，导致光瞳被分割，在单共轭自适应光学中产生差分活塞（petal 模式）。可见光波段下金字塔波前传感器对这些模式灵敏度差，残差湍流条件下差分活塞会累积漂移（Island Effect），使 PSF 退化甚至失锁。已有的空间缓解手段（如 Edge Actuator Coupling）会带来 Strehl 损失，因此需要一种不损害连续模式校正的轻量控制器。

Method: 提出 MMSE Soft-Limiter：一个确定性、轻量的控制器，由两部分组成——(1) 基于 von Kármán 先验导出的子空间 MMSE 估计器；(2) 连续时间正则化，形式上等价于仅作用于相对 petal 自由度的泄漏积分器（leaky integrator）。采用 SPECULA 进行 ELT/ANDES 的端到端仿真验证，并理论推导泄漏增益的工作窗口 g ≲ g_eff/π。

Result: 仿真显示：在 seeing ≥ 1.0'' 时，经典积分器会失锁并在多个条纹间游走；而 MMSE Soft-Limiter 在 0.6–1.2'' 范围内将差分活塞限制在单个条纹内，在强湍流下约束了 petal 偏移及其对科学波段的影响；在良好 seeing 下保持最优连续模式校正，且无 Edge Actuator Coupling 式的 Strehl 惩罚。理论推导与验证表明，在 g ≲ g_eff/π 以下快速环路可保留至 λ_WFS/2 的真实物理台阶（仅有小幅 droop），从而在快速传感器上维持稳定的模 λ_WFS 波前。

Conclusion: MMSE Soft-Limiter 提供了一种无 Strehl 代价的 petal 模式稳定方案，使快速环保持在模 λ_WFS 波前上，从而允许较慢的 phasing 传感器解算整数模糊。随着湍流增强，工作窗口收窄并在约 1.0'' seeing 处边界交叉；超过该点，保留物理不连续性的责任完全转移给 phasing 通道，这成为分段式 ELT 的关键设计驱动因素。

Abstract: Extremely large telescopes (ELTs) rely on segmented mirrors and thick support structures that fragment the pupil. In single-conjugate AO (SCAO), this produces differential pistons, or petal modes. The Pyramid Wavefront Sensor (PWFS), baseline for ANDES, MICADO, HARMONI, and GMT-NGSAO, has poor sensitivity to these modes in the visible under realistic residual turbulence. We aim to prevent the "Island Effect", where poorly observed differential pistons accumulate and degrade the PSF, without compromising continuous-mode correction. We propose the Minimum Mean Square Error (MMSE) Soft-Limiter, a deterministic, lightweight controller combining a subspace MMSE estimator derived from von Kármán priors with a continuous temporal regularization. It acts as a leaky integrator applied only to the relative petal degrees of freedom. End-to-end SPECULA simulations of ELT/ANDES show that, while a classical integrator loses lock and wanders across multiple fringes at seeing $\ge 1.0''$, the MMSE Soft-Limiter confines the differential piston within a single fringe over the $0.6-1.2''$ range. It bounds the petal excursion and its science-band impact under severe turbulence, while keeping optimal continuous-mode correction at good seeing, without the Strehl penalty of spatial mitigation such as Edge Actuator Coupling. We derive and verify the operating window for the leakage gain, $g \lesssim g_{\mathrm{eff}}/π$: below it, the fast loop retains genuine physical steps up to $λ_{WFS}/2$ with a small droop. This maintains a stable modulo-$λ_{WFS}$ wavefront on the fast sensor, letting a slower phasing sensor resolve integer ambiguities. As the window narrows with turbulence, its boundaries cross near $1.0''$ seeing; beyond this, retaining physical discontinuities becomes the sole responsibility of the phasing channel, a key design driver for segmented ELTs.

</details>


### [109] [Model-Based Retrieval of Four Years of JWST/NIRCam Wavefronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40187)
*Shrishmoy Ray,Benjamin Pope,Louis Desdoigts,Hayden Greer,Milo Langker,Peter Tuthill,Marshall Perrin,Anand Sivaramakrishnan,Alexandra Greenbaum,Laurent Pueyo,Max Charles*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 提出开源算法 camino，用可微光学模型与正则化极大似然推断高精度重建 JWST/NIRCam 波前。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST 数据保真度依赖对镜面拼接与对齐的精确监测，目前利用 NIRCam 周期获取的离焦参考星图像、由闭源相位反演软件推断波前，社区无法访问且难以嵌入科学分析流程，因此需要高精度、开源、可集成的波前估计方法。

Method: 构建 camino（Computational Aberration Modelling and Inference for NIRCam Observations）：基于 JAX 与可微 Fresnel 光学代码 dLUX 建立 NIRCam 前向模型，采用正则化极大似然推断，并以全部 NIRCam 波前传感档案数据作为条件进行标定。

Result: 对离焦数据的拟合优度（约化卡方）由 60–65 改善至 12–13；重建了自科学运行开始至 2026 年初的 JWST 波前历史；该开源实现可并行于 STScI 现有相位反演算法运行。

Conclusion: 该方法向科学界开放高精度波前估计能力，便于直接嵌入科学分析工作流，并朝基于物理前向建模的预测式定标、免除耗时同期参考星观测的最终目标迈出重要一步。

Abstract: The fidelity of data recovered by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) depends on precise monitoring of the mirror segments' surfaces and alignment. To this end, every few days the observatory takes pairs of defocused images of a reference star with its NIRCam instrument; a closed-source phase retrieval package is then employed to infer the wavefront. We here present camino (Computational Aberration Modelling and Inference for NIRCam Observations), an open-source algorithm to model NIRCam built in JAX using the differentiable Fresnel optics code dLUX and regularised maximum likelihood inference that has been conditioned on the entire archive of NIRCam wavefront sensing data. We typically improve the reduced chi-squared in fits to the defocused data from 60-65 to 12-13. We reconstruct the wavefront history of JWST from the beginning of science operations to early 2026. This open source implementation operates in parallel to the current STScI phase retrieval algorithm, allowing access by the broader scientific community to high-accuracy wavefront estimation and enabling its direct integration into science-analysis workflows. This represents a major step towards the ultimate goal of predictive calibration methods based on physics-based forward modelling, obviating the need for time-consuming observation of contemporaneous dedicated reference stars.

</details>


### [110] [posterior-landscape: morphological feature finding in two-dimensional probability densities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40272)
*Michele Mancarella,Viola De Renzis,Alessandro Agapito*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 提出 Python 框架 posterior-landscape，融合拓扑数据分析、统计密度脊估计与尺度空间图像分析，用于识别和刻画二维概率密度中的形态特征，并在贝叶斯反演中传播特征位置、几何与对应关系的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维概率密度（尤其是贝叶斯后验）中常含局部聚集、谷、脊和斜率变化等结构，但缺乏系统化、可量化且能传播不确定性的识别与刻画工具；同时这些二维结构与其一维边缘分布特征的关系、以及它们与其他推断参数的相关性也缺少统一处理方式。

Method: 结合三类方法：拓扑数据分析、统计密度脊估计、尺度空间（scale-space）图像分析。框架提取局部集中区、谷、脊与斜率变化；对贝叶斯重建，传播特征恢复、几何形态与特征对应关系（correspondence）在后验上的不确定性；并将二维结构与一维边缘分布中的特征关联起来，量化其与其他推断参数的相关性。在受控密度上做验证，并应用于引力波族群重建。

Result: 在受控（已知真值）密度上验证了方法的有效性；在引力波族群重建中系统性识别并解释族群特征，判断哪些特征携带与宇宙学相关的信息，并给出可与其他推断参数对比的形态学目录。

Conclusion: posterior-landscape 提供了从二维后验中系统提取、不确定性感知的形态特征描述的统一框架，其输出目录可作为未来理论预言与族群重建之间比较的通用形态学表示。

Abstract: We present posterior-landscape, a Python framework to identify and characterize morphological features in two-dimensional probability densities. The framework combines concepts from topological data analysis, statistical density-ridge estimation, and scale-space image analysis. It extracts localized concentrations, valleys, ridges, and changes of slope; for Bayesian reconstructions, it propagates uncertainty in feature recovery, geometry, and correspondence across posteriors. The framework also connects two-dimensional structures to features in one-dimensional marginals, and quantifies their correlation with other inferred parameters. We validate the method on controlled densities, and demonstrate its use on gravitational-wave population reconstructions, where it provides a systematic way to identify and interpret population features and assess which ones carry information relevant for cosmology. The resulting catalogues also offer a common morphological representation for future comparisons between predictions and population reconstructions.

</details>


### [111] [Tailoring Infra-Red Filters for Global Mapping of Enceladus' Thermal Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40456)
*D. G. Lyster,C. J. A. Howett,N. E. Bowles,K. Nowicki,R. Evans,T. J. Warren*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出土卫二热成像仪(ETM)的设计与可行性初步研究：以384×288像素热红外传感器在150 km轨道高度实现80 m/像素的连续高分辨率温度测绘，覆盖低至45 K的极区冬温，用于约束土卫二全球传导热损失并刻画其南极活动地形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土卫二因具有次表层海洋与活跃冰火山活动而被Cassini任务列为天体生物学重点目标。但Cassini以土星为中心运行，对土卫二的高分辨率飞越数据十分有限，缺乏覆盖全球、尤其是极区极端低温（可达45 K）的系统性温度数据，限制了对全球传导热损失与南极活动地形热状态的认识。

Method: 基于TechDemoSat-1、Mars Climate Sounder与Lunar Trailblazer等任务的技术继承，设计轻量、低功耗的ETM仪器；采用384×288像素热传感器，在150 km轨道高度实现80 m/像素分辨率；并通过建立滤光组件数字模型来优化热辐射测量谱段。

Result: 完成ETM的初步可行性评估与设计，实现环土卫二轨道上的连续高分辨率热测绘能力，较Cassini飞越数据在覆盖范围与分辨率上显著提升；滤光片数字模型为热辐射测量谱段优化提供了依据。

Conclusion: ETM能够改进对土卫二全球传导热损失的约束，更细致地刻画并给出南极活动地形（South Polar Terrain）的热学背景，为理解其地质行为、演化历史与潜在宜居性提供关键温度数据。

Abstract: We present the results of an initial study into the feasibility and design of the Enceladus Thermal Mapper (ETM) instrument, designed to provide detailed temperature data for Enceladus, an icy moon of Saturn identified by the Cassini Mission as a prime candidate for astrobiological study due to its subsurface ocean and active cryovolcanism. Building on heritage from previous missions including TechDemoSat-1, Mars Climate Sounder, and Lunar Trailblazer, ETM aims to measure temperature variations across Enceladus' surface, including at the poles during the extreme cold of winter, when they can reach as low as 45 K. Where Cassini provided limited high-resolution flyby data of Enceladus due to its Saturn-centric orbit, ETM, in orbit around the icy moon will offer continuous high-resolution thermal mapping with a 384 x 288-pixel sensor, capable of 80 m/pixel resolution from 150 km altitude. This will improve constraints on the Enceladus' global conductive heat loss, and better characterise and contextualise its active South Polar Terrain. To optimise the instrument's filters for thermal emission measurements, a digital model of the filter assembly was developed. This lightweight, low-power instrument is capable of significantly advancing the understanding of Enceladus' geological behaviour and evaluating its potential habitability by providing comprehensive temperature data essential for interpreting its evolution.

</details>


### [112] [Measuring Precipitable Water Vapor through Dual-Band GPS at Rubin Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40363)
*Anja Weyant,W. M. Wood-Vasey,Elana K. Urbach,Sara Gelven,Marcos Tamargo-Arizmedi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该论文利用GNSS信号延迟在Rubin天文台连续监测可降水量（PWV），验证精度达0.9 kg/m²，并给出季节与日变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地基天文观测中，大气可降水量（PWV）是系统性误差来源，尤其在大于7000埃波长处会造成与波长相关的消光，影响超新星宇宙学等高精度应用，因此需要逐次观测精确测定透过率函数；即使在南美干燥的安第斯山区，水汽吸收仍存在时变影响。

Method: 利用全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）信号延迟反演PWV，实现24小时稳健监测；先通过与IGS结果比对验证分析流程一致性，并在Allegheny天文台用Trimble Net R9接收机完成调试运行后移至Rubin天文台，自2025年9月5日起连续测量。

Result: Rubin天文台春季（9—11月）平均PWV为3.0 kg/m²，夏季（12—2月）为5.4 kg/m²；PWV存在明显日变化，振幅1.2—3.8 kg/m²，且与东西向风速高度相关。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于GNSS的处理方法达到0.9 kg/m²的准确度与0.36 kg/m²的精密度，具备提供连续、高时间分辨率PWV监测的能力，可支撑LSST高要求科学目标。

Abstract: Precipitable water vapor (PWV) in Earth's atmosphere is a known source of systematic error in ground-based astronomical measurements, particularly at wavelengths greater than 7,000 angstroms. PWV produces wavelength-dependent extinction in the spectral energy distributions of observed sources. For high-precision applications, such as supernova cosmology, the transmission function must be measured accurately and precisely for each observation. The Vera C. Rubin Observatory is located in the Chilean Andes, a region known for dry conditions. However, even at this location, time-variable absorption by water vapor is a factor. Signal delays from Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) offer a robust, easy, 24-hour monitoring of PWV. In this paper we present measurements from the first six months of GNSS-PWV at Rubin Observatory. We verified our analysis by replicating results from the International GNSS Service (IGS), demonstrating high consistency. We then installed and commissioned a Trimble Net R9 receiver at Allegheny Observatory for a commissioning run before it was relocated to Rubin Observatory. At Rubin Observatory we have been continuously measuring PWV since 2025 September 5. We find average PWV levels of 3.0 kg/m$^2$ in Southern Hemisphere spring (September to November) and 5.4 kg/m$^2$ in summer (December to February). The PWV at Rubin also has a clear daily variation with an amplitude of 1.2-3.8 kg/m$^2$ that is highly correlated with the east-west wind strength. We conclude that the proposed GNSS-based processing method achieves an accuracy of 0.9 kg/m$^2$ with 0.36 kg/m$^2$ precision, confirming the capability for continuous, high-temporal-resolution PWV monitoring to support demanding LSST science cases.

</details>
